## Numerology 1 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 1

ARCHETYPE NAME:

The role model

The number 1 resonates with the the Sun.

The Numerology of 1: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 1 in numerology is often associated with leadership, ambition, and self-reliance, embodying qualities reminiscent of the Sun's life-giving and illuminating properties. This number symbolizes the beginning, encapsulating the essence of drive, vitality, and an unyielding zest for life. It radiates with optimism and the fervor to excel, fostering a spirit of leadership and a profound inner need for independence and autonomy. When the energy of number 1 is harnessed positively, it sparks creativity and innovation, much like a river's relentless flow demanding freedom, movement, and expression.

Individuals influenced by the number 1, such as those born on the 1st of any month or whose life path number reduces to 1, often carry an innate sense of confidence, akin to wearing an invisible "gold medal." They possess a strong belief in their capabilities and frequently seek affirmation and recognition from others. Flattery can be particularly effective with these individuals, as they cherish being acknowledged for their efforts and talents.

However, engaging in dialogue with someone resonant with the number 1 can sometimes be challenging due to their inherent stubbornness. This trait can make them reluctant to deviate from their viewpoints or admit mistakes, which might be perceived as a sign of defeat. This stubbornness can sometimes lead others to perceive them as distant or arrogant.

A penchant for luxury often characterizes those influenced by the number 1. They may have a fondness for high-end clothing, gourmet dining, and luxury vehicles, alongside a propensity for investing in expensive accessories and jewelry. For instance, many men resonant with the number 1 might prefer gold watches, rings, and chains, demonstrating their taste for opulence.

The number 1 is adept at conceptualizing ideas and often excels in environments where they can lead and delegate more labor-intensive tasks to others. They are natural at attracting luck, as they embody the pioneering spirit of new beginnings. However, encountering periods of diminished luck or obstacles can be particularly challenging for them.

In professional realms, the number 1 is synonymous with entrepreneurship and leadership. Individuals influenced by this number are driven to excel and often rise to prominent positions in various fields, including politics, business, law, and other leadership roles.

The primary emotional challenges for someone resonant with the number 1 include tendencies towards vanity, arrogance, and narcissism. They may struggle with accepting advice or feedback, and an imbalanced expression of this energy can manifest as dictatorial, stubborn, and overly perfectionistic traits. Their aversion to losing can stem from a deep-seated need to be the best, as their identity is closely tied to their achievements.

When balanced, the number 1 connects deeply with its source energy, embodying boundless power and enthusiasm. Like the Sun, which remains constant despite the changing clouds, a balanced number 1 maintains its luminosity and strength, undeterred by fluctuating circumstances or emotions. This balance allows them to shine with a steadfast and radiant inner power.

Tarot card for 1 "The Magician"

The number 1 corresponds to the first card in the Tarot called "The Magician."

The Magician has one hand pointing upwards towards the sky. The other hand points down to the earth. This represents "as above, so below." On the table in front of him are a cup, a sword, a wand, and a pentacle, representing the four suits of the Minor Arcana. They symbolize the classical elements of earth, air, fire, and water. This suggests that the Magician can harness the powers of these elements. The Magician holds a double-ended white wand representing a conduit between the spiritual and the physical (also a symbol of the number 1). His robe is also white, a symbol of purity yet also of inexperience, while his red mantle symbolizes willpower and passion, but also egotism, rage, and revenge.

The Magician symbolizes the power of the number 1. It can tap into divine powers and initiate things in the physical world. To see an in depth description of this tarot card please read this article:

## Numerology 2 Meaning
Planet moon associated with the numerology of 2

The romantic creative

The number 2 resonates with the the Moon.

The Numerology of 2: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 2 in numerology is synonymous with receptivity, creativity, imagination, and empathy. It is represented by the Moon, embodying the qualities of the water element, the subconscious mind, and deep emotions. Those influenced by the number 2 tend to be sensitive, open-hearted, and readily affected by the emotions of others. They are known for their gentleness, charm, honesty, and loving nature, coupled with an empathetic disposition and compassionate hearts.

Individuals under the number 2's influence often find their mood and state of mind closely tied to the Moon's phases. They might experience mood swings akin to a pendulum, oscillating between emotional extremes. For instance, the full Moon may heighten primal desires or over-emotionality, while the new Moon might inspire elevated states of consciousness.

Number 2 individuals thrive in harmonious environments. Dissonance or discord in their surroundings can lead to discomfort, possibly manifesting as headaches, migraines, or other physical ailments. They seek peace and beauty in their environment.

Throughout their lives, those influenced by the number 2 retain a childlike charm and often show disinterest in mundane duties and routines. They might struggle with concentration and grounding, and sometimes procrastinate. However, they excel in flowing with life's rhythm, effortlessly attracting abundance, prosperity, and social connections. Their creative expression blossoms naturally, and they enjoy social events, parties, and entertaining others.

People with a number 2 energy gravitate towards careers that involve entertainment or helping others. They might find fulfillment in acting, circus roles, stand-up comedy, or supportive roles like assistants, coaches, or customer service. Working in teams and being part of a community brings them joy.

The Moon's nature to reflect the Sun's light symbolizes the number 2's deep longing for unity and connection. This makes them inherently romantic, dreaming of an ideal partner to lead them back to a sense of oneness. Number 2 individuals are sensitive and passionate lovers, quickly falling in love but prone to dependency and idealization of their partners. They seek a partner who can fulfill their romantic ideals.

The major challenge for a number 2 is to balance their innate sensitivity with self-awareness, distinguishing their own emotions from those they absorb from others. They may often feel a sense of disconnection, yearning for a greater sense of wholeness.

When unbalanced, a number 2 might become dismissive and rude, internally struggling due to their actions. They may be overly sensitive, take things personally, and become overly accommodating, craving attention and approval. There's also a tendency to excessively devote themselves to a cause or community.

As a balanced 2, there's the potential to develop an almost telepathic level of empathy and compassion, enabling an intuitive understanding of others' thoughts and feelings.

Tarot card for 2 "The High Priestess"

The number 2 corresponds to the second card in the Tarot, "The High Priestess."

The High Priestess symbolizes the feminine presence of the divine.

She wears blue robes and sits with her hands in her lap. She has a lunar crescent at her feet, "a horned diadem on her head, with a globe on her head, similar to the crown of the ancient Egyptian goddess Hathor.

She has a large cross on her breast, balancing the four elements: fire, water, earth, and air.

The scroll in her hands, partly covered by her mantle, bears the letters TORA (meaning "divine law"), which symbolizes the memory we carry inside about the past, present, and future.

She is seated between the white and black pillars—'J' and 'B' for Jachin and Boaz—of the mystic Temple of Solomon. The veil of the Temple is behind her: it is embroidered with palm leaves (male) and pomegranates (female), growing on a tree-shaped like the tree of life. 2 is under the influence of the MOON

The moon is feminine and reflective. It reflects the Sun's light and brings light to places that otherwise would be dark.

The moon's cycles are very obvious to us here on earth. The moon always influences our moods - moods that swing between volatile and unpredictable (when it is a full moon) or inward going and transcendent when it is a new moon.

People of the Moon are emotional people with many mood swings who readily adapt to their surroundings and circumstances. The moon resonance gives artistic talent and creativity. There is a complex inner world with a fertile imagination.

They achieve their goals by going around or avoiding obstacles and intuitively using favorable opportunities.

Moon people are always unconsciously looking for a "sun" person who can shine their light on them and who they can rely on.

They are family-oriented, loyal, sensitive, and don't take many risks.

Moon people are excellent diplomats as they are soft-spoken.

## Numerology 3 Meaning
Planet jupiter associated with the numerology of 3

The Strategist

The number 3 resonates with the the planet Jupiter.

The Numerology of 3: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 3 is associated with strength, confidence, and the capacity for expansion, reflecting the characteristics of Jupiter, the largest planet in our solar system. This number is all about grandeur and growth, emitting a significant amount of energy into its surroundings. People influenced by the number 3 are often ambitious, focused on their goals, dynamic, and self-assured. They set high standards for themselves and others, viewing success as a paramount objective.

3 also possesses a vibrant sense of humor and thoroughly enjoys fun times with friends and family.

Often regarded as the most potent of all numerological digits, the number 3 bears resemblance to the Roman God Jupiter. Like Jupiter, with its 69 moons and central role in its celestial family, the number 3 exudes power and demands respect. Those under its influence aim for perfection in every aspect of life and are quick to assert their boundaries and reclaim their status if crossed. The number 3's power stems from a strong strategic mindset, capable of excelling in various fields, from academic pursuits to strategy-based games like chess.

However, individuals with a strong 3 energy may tend to create facades, presenting only the most polished aspects of their lives to the world. This tendency can sometimes lead to a disconnection from their true emotions.

One of the key lessons for the number 3 is to understand that life is a series of learning experiences, not just a succession of wins or losses. Acknowledging that life has its ups and downs and focusing on personal growth can be more fulfilling than merely acquiring external symbols of success.

Those with the number 3 energy have a natural talent for manifesting their desires and achieving their dreams. When balanced, they are ambitious and driven, yet they also make time to enjoy life's pleasures with friends and family.

Number 3 individuals often seek roles of authority and influence, finding comfort in leadership

positions, including directorships or politics, where they can lead without letting emotions cloud their judgment.

When the 3 energy is blocked, it can lead to a drop in self-esteem and a victim mentality, where envy and blame projection may arise. In contrast, an overpowering 3 energy might lead to emotional shutdown, cynicism, arrogance, and a dogged pursuit of goals, regardless of the means. This imbalance can result in a competitive, uncompassionate, and judgmental disposition.

The fully realized 3 energy attains success and greatness that resonate with the authenticity and magnificence of Jupiter, achieving a harmonious blend of ambition and genuine connection.

Tarot card for 3 "The Empress"

The number 3 corresponds with the 3rd Tarot card called "The Empress".

The Empress sits on a throne wearing a crown with twelve stars, holding a scepter in one hand. The scepter is representative of her power over life, her crown has twelve stars representing her dominance over the year, and her throne is in the midst of a field of grain, representative of her dominion over growing things. The Empress is representative of the productivity of the subconscious, seeded by ideas. She is meant to be the embodiment of the growth of the natural world, fertility, and what one knows or believes from the heart.

3 is under the influence of JUPITER

Jupiter is the largest planet in the solar system; it is expansive and powerful and has a considerable family of moons around it. It is like a solar system within our solar system.

Jupiter is a patriarch and loves to have family and life around it.

Jupiter is the "loudest" planet. When the satellite Voyager passed Jupiter, all its meters and measurement devices went haywire due to the enormous amounts of electromagnetic noise that Jupiter exudes. Jupiter has a massive field of information around it that extends thousands of kilometers into the surrounding space. These Jupiter traits make the Jupiter person speak loudly, act confidently, love humor, and literally be "jovial" (the word jovial means "Jupiter-like"). The Jupiter person is, in general, an extroverted personality that loves to take up space.

Simultaneously, Jupiter is also full of "gas" and can "puff itself up," making itself appear larger or more substantial than it is.

Jupiter people are optimistic people who like to engage with others. They give a lot and expect a lot in return. Interchange has to be significant and meaningful.

Jupiter demands respect, and Jupiter people are very status aware.

## Numerology 4 Meaning
Planet Uranus associated with the numerology of 4

The Builder

The number 4 resonates with the the planet Uranus.

The Numerology of 4: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 4 in numerology is emblematic of the capacity to build anew and think creatively, both inside and outside conventional frameworks. This number is linked to Uranus, a planet distinguished in the solar system by its unique horizontal axis of rotation. Like Uranus, which rolls along its orbit, individuals influenced by the number 4 possess a distinctiveness in their musical tastes, fashion sense, and life perspectives.

Uranus experiences prolonged winters lasting 21 years, symbolizing the depth, thoroughness, and thoughtfulness of the number 4. This profound approach is often the bedrock of innovation, and many notable scientists and geniuses have a strong presence of 4 in their numerological charts. The transition of Uranus from winter to spring, marked by intense upheavals and storms, parallels the transformative experiences in the lives of those under the number 4's influence, often involving significant changes and starting anew.

Individuals resonating with the number 4 can exhibit stark contrasts within themselves. They can be gentle and sensitive yet possess a robust survival instinct, sometimes bordering on opportunism.

The number 4 is associated with the square – stable and strong. However, alongside this desire for stability, there often lies a hidden urge to dismantle and rebuild from scratch. This trait is particularly pronounced in variants like 13/4 and 31/4.

This propensity to challenge established structures can give those influenced by the number 4 a rebellious, anti-authoritarian streak. They often go against the consensus, which can lead to feelings of isolation or being the 'black sheep'.

Uranus is the quietest planet, and similarly, a person with the 4 energy may not be overt about their talents or successes, contrasting with the more demonstrative energies of Jupiter or the Sun.

The 4 energy fosters a deep, sincere drive to establish new structures and frameworks, reflecting a pioneering spirit coupled with a practical, get-things-done attitude. It also imbues great perseverance, enabling the completion of tasks others might shy away from. While variants like 13 and 31 might lean more towards mental and contrarian tendencies, the pure number 4 is the most steadfast and stable of them.

A critical life lesson for the number 4 is to open up to humanity and find a sense of belonging. When the 4 energy is suppressed, it can lead to feelings of alienation, dissatisfaction, bitterness, and a sense of meaninglessness. Conversely, an excess of this energy can manifest in rebelliousness without a clear purpose.

The ultimate calling of the number 4 is to introduce innovative ideas, improve the world, and develop new structures and systems. It resonates with the energies of universal inspiration and has access to higher mental planes, where groundbreaking ideas are conceived.

Tarot card for 4 "The Emperor"

The number 4 corresponds to the 4th card in the Tarot called "The Emperor."

The Emperor is sitting on a throne adorned with two ram heads. There is also a ram head on his left shoulder.

His long white beard symbolizes wisdom. He holds a globe in his left hand and an Ankh in his right hand. The mountain behind him is barren, representing that you need to remove emotion to govern.

The Emperor symbolizes the "solidity" aspect of the number 4, which builds solid things up in the world. To see an in depth description of this tarot card please read this article:

IN DEPTH WITH TAROT CARD 4

The 4 in balance is:

Solid

Pioneering

4 is under the influence of URANUS

Uranus is the coldest planet in our solar system and has a temperature of about -220 degrees centigrade. There are large cloud formations in the atmosphere, created by water in the lowest clouds and methane in the upper cloud cover. The planet itself is made up of ice and rocks. The clouds give a cloudy light blue impenetrable look. Much like the planet itself, Uranus people are mysterious, profound and don't reveal much about themselves at first glance.

Uranus is the quietest of all planets. In contrast to this, Jupiter makes a lot of electromagnetic noise as you approach it.

Uranus has large, vertical rings around it. Where Saturn's rings are horizontal, Uranus' rings are vertical. This symbolizes an opposition between the two planets.

The axis of Uranus is tilted almost 90 degrees. This makes Uranus "roll" along its axis, while the other planets are like spinning tops on their trajectories. This makes Uranus unique among all the worlds. In Uranus people, this translates to them doing and saying the opposite of what everyone else is saying. There is both an excentric and contrarian streak.

It takes Uranus a full 84 years to orbit the sun, and the eccentric axis of rotation means that while one hemisphere is bathed in light, the other is in constant darkness - for an entire season. This gives very long summers and winters. The transition between summer and winter happens very quickly on Uranus, symbolizing upheavals, and violent transformations. That is why Uranus represents changes that occur very suddenly, and that changes a person, a generation, or the whole world.

The long winter represents that Uranus energy goes in-depth. It is not fast and flighty but thorough and profound.

The north and south poles are not fixed, which means that Uranus does not stand firm on how things are, and the truth is perceived as relative and can be continually challenged.

## Numerology 5 Meaning
Planet mercury associated with the numerology of 5

The Communicator

The number 5 resonates with the the planet Mercury.

The Numerology of 5: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 5 vibrates with the energies of agility, communication, networking, and the creation of new business ventures. Representing Mercury, the swiftly orbiting planet that completes a journey around the Sun in just 88 days, the number 5 embodies quick perception and the ability to view situations from multiple perspectives. Constant movement and energetic activity are inherent to this number. Individuals influenced by the number 5 are lovers of life and action, always engaged in something and finding ease in initiating conversations with new people.

As a 5, you are known for your spontaneity, persuasive skills, and agile mind. Those with a 5 energy are adept at sales, networking, and public speaking, radiating enthusiasm and charisma. They easily make friends, approach life with an egalitarian mindset, and have a warm, inviting personality. They thrive on exciting social interactions, new adventures, and constant change. Skilled at multitasking and generating ideas, a 5, however, can sometimes take on more than they can manage. Their talkative nature can occasionally be perceived as overbearing or self-centered.

The number 5 is innately curious and explorative, making careers that satisfy this thirst for knowledge and adventure particularly suitable. They excel in business, deal-making, sales, marketing, and fields related to communication, people, and media.

5's can sometimes be seen as fickle due to their propensity to swiftly move through life's experiences, including relationships. Their restless nature and desire for change can be challenging for partners who seek stability and reflection.

A significant life lesson for a 5 is to carefully select from their plethora of ideas and cultivate the discipline to follow through with them. When the energy of a 5 is blocked, they can feel trapped, become introspective, overrun by incessant thoughts, and struggle with decision-making. This often leads to much talk but little action. Conversely, when the 5 energy is excessive, it manifests as hyperactivity, impulsivity, irresponsibility, and potential burnout. This state can lead to superficial interactions and constantly changing opinions and plans.

Balanced 5's are joyful, spontaneous, witty, and charming, always growing and evolving.

The greatest strength and potential of a 5 lie in their communicative abilities and talent for connecting people. Spiritually, the 5 functions as an alchemist, bridging the physical, etheric, astral, mental, and causal planes. This unique capability enables them to quickly grasp complex concepts and articulate them in ways that are comprehensible, marketable, and brandable.

## Numerology 6 Meaning
Planet venus associated with the numerology of 6

The Lover

The number 6 resonates with the the planet Venus.

The Numerology of 6: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 6 is associated with beauty, harmony, charisma, and the ability to experience deep love. Under the influence of Venus, individuals resonating with number 6 possess a magnetic charm and an effortless ability to attract others. They are often sensual and excel at flirting, exuding a natural allure. The 6 values home life, comfort, and a harmonious atmosphere. It embodies caring qualities, enjoys tranquility, and upholds traditions. With its presence, the 6 can create a positive mood and vibe within a community, fostering a sense of togetherness.

The 6 has a strong inclination to assist others and is characterized by loyalty, faithfulness, helpfulness, duty, and love. However, it can also be stubborn, holding firmly to its own opinions. This trait can lead to emotional dramas and family conflicts, and at times, the 6 may manipulate others emotionally.

A love for home and familiarity marks the 6, along with a deep sense of patriotism. Yet, in an unbalanced state, this patriotism can transform into nationalism and xenophobia.

Central to the life of a 6 are relationships, friendships, and family. The number 6 epitomizes love in its myriad forms – ranging from sensuality and eroticism, romantic love, familial affection, self-love, to the divine love of 'Agape.' They are willing to go to great lengths for their loved ones, though they may occasionally fall into martyr or victim roles, feeling unappreciated or overly burdened. The nurturing nature of the 6 often leads them to caregiving professions, such as nursing or medicine.

The 6 is naturally inclined to be a conduit of divine beauty and harmony, often expressed through the arts like music, theater, opera, and dance. Many great artists in history have been born on dates with a digit sum of 6, according to Numerologist PRO Statistics.

When the energy of 6 is blocked, there may be feelings of being unloved or unable to love, coupled with a victim mentality in relationships. An excessive 6 energy can lead to unforgiveness, hardness, and holding onto grudges. This overabundance can also manifest in manipulation for security, love, or sexual gratification. In extreme imbalances, the 6 may become devious, cunning, and overly manipulative, resorting to drama and gossip for emotional blackmail.

The key life lessons for a 6 involve navigating relationships, managing emotions, and establishing boundaries. It's important for them to appreciate themselves and not solely focus on helping others. The 6 must also learn to respect others' limits with their well-meaning assistance and avoid becoming overly dependent or attached in romantic relationships.

The 6 energy has the ability to channel its innate, refined sexuality and sensuality as a means to higher consciousness, fully awakening the heart chakra. This potential allows for the manifestation of an unlimited, boundless love.

## Numerology 7 Meaning
Planet neptune associated with the numerology of 7

The Seeker

The number 7 resonates with the the planet Neptune.

The Numerology of 7: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 7 imparts a deep personality, keen observation skills, analytical abilities, and a natural inclination towards spirituality. It resonates with the energy of Neptune, a mysterious and stunningly beautiful planet with a dream-like quality. As the farthest planet from the Sun in our solar system, Neptune is closely linked to the vastness of the cosmos. It symbolizes intuition, spirituality, clairvoyance, and telepathy, representing the deep currents of the subconscious mind. Neptune, named after the Roman counterpart to the Greek god Poseidon, is associated with the subconscious and the element of water, hinting at possible hidden water beneath its thick gaseous atmosphere. This planetary energy fosters a belief in universal equality and love for all beings and cultures, transcending local biases.

Possessing multiple 7s in your numerology chart signifies a profound, introspective, and intuitive nature. You see the world in a contemplative light, seeking deeper truths. As someone who is idealistic and not materialistically driven, you see yourself as a global citizen, embracing people of all races, religions, and affiliations. This perspective enables you to excel in international careers, businesses, and networks, valuing wisdom over material gain.

The 7 thrives in abstract, high-level thinking, typically finding mundane tasks less appealing.

As a 7, you have a clear understanding of your life’s mission and a deep sense of self-awareness. Renowned for your generosity, romanticism, and popularity, 7s attract others seeking guidance with their wisdom. They tend to be liberal, adventurous, creative, and imaginative, often excelling in the arts and sciences.

Like Neptune, which harbors fierce surface storms but harbors stillness within, a 7 may appear intense, anxious, or introspective, yet internally, they possess a reservoir of profound wisdom and tranquility. This dichotomy lends a 7 an air of mystery, often perceived as transcendent or otherworldly.

Under the influence of the number 7, you perceive the interconnectedness of all things, eschewing black-and-white judgments in favor of deeper insights.

In relationships, 7s are receptive and can be easily flattered, but may struggle with feeling present with others. They often become introverted, immersed in their inner world, which can be puzzling to their partners.

7s are commonly drawn to professions such as artistry, music, writing, science, or law, driven by a desire to understand reality at a deeper level.

A balanced 7 is insightful, profound, and an expert in their chosen field.

When the 7 energy is blocked, it can lead to insecurity, mental instability, procrastination, avoidance of stress, and susceptibility to substance abuse as an escape. Conversely, an excess of 7 energy can manifest as selfishness, criticality, restlessness, and skepticism, closing off spirituality and inner life.

A key life lesson for the 7 is to ground themselves and believe in their ideas enough to actualize them. The 7's ultimate purpose is to enlighten others with the universal wisdom at its core. Spiritually, it offers a connection to the universal mind and a deeper divine reality, potentially developing extraordinary sensory abilities and the power to heal others in spirit, mind, and body.

## Numerology 8 Meaning
Planet Saturn associated with the numerology of 8

The Advisor

The number 8 resonates with the the planet Saturn.

The Numerology of 8: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 8 embodies strength, the ability to harmonize energies, and the wisdom to judiciously assess any situation. It is in harmony with the planet Saturn, known for its distinctive rings and the enigmatic hexagon at its north pole, making it one of the solar system's more unusual planets. Saturn aligns with the earth element and was revered in Roman mythology as the god of harvest, and in Greek mythology as Kronos, a deity who reigned during a time of natural harmony.

Individuals influenced by the number 8 possess a robust personality, exceptional survival skills, and a relentless spirit in the face of adversity. They are characterized by their focus, ambition, and sharp intellect, often planning meticulously in their minds before actualizing their visions. Being associated with Saturn, a planet symbolizing fate and karma, those under the influence of 8 often face significant challenges, yet these hardships imbue them with formidable resilience.

The symbol of the number 8 mirrors that of infinity, suggesting a resonance with the concept of eternity. This lends a lasting impact to those with 8 energy, often leading them to be remembered long after their passing.

An individual with the number 8 can be simultaneously passionate, organized, and deeply spiritual or driven by a higher calling. When balanced, 8 combines its spirituality with practicality. However, in imbalance, 8 may focus excessively on either the spiritual or material aspects of life, leading to either materialism akin to an unbalanced number 3, or excessive mentality or spirituality, similar to an unbalanced number 7.

8's, akin to Saturn, are distinct and unconventional, charting their own paths and remaining unfazed by others' opinions.

A balanced 8 is warm, passionate, self-assured, and characterized by honesty and loyalty.

Internally, 8's may struggle with feelings of misunderstanding and loneliness, preferring solitude. They often view seeking help as a sign of weakness and may conceal their emotions, appearing cold or cynical to outsiders. However, they possess a generous heart and strong moral compass, and once they feel respected, they are incredibly dedicated to others. Respect is a critical element for them.

8's are naturally drawn to roles such as philosophers, artists, freedom fighters, religious leaders, judges, politicians, or administrators.

When the energy of 8 is blocked, there's a tendency towards insecurity and fear of self-expression. Analogous to Saturn surrounded by its rings, 8's might create invisible barriers to keep others at a distance, protecting their emotional and physical wellbeing. This can lead to significant self-esteem and self-confidence challenges. Conversely, when the energy of 8 is excessive, it may manifest as irritability, impatience, verbal aggression, and even a brutal, Machiavellian nature.

For those with life path number 8, your mission is to blend your inherent spirituality with your practical strengths, synthesizing the ethereal with the earthly. Your foremost life lesson involves learning forgiveness, trusting in life's journey, and recognizing your rightful place in the world.

The energy of Saturn demands material well being and certainty of the future.

Saturn people both give and demand loyalty, and they are very reliable people.

## Numerology 9 Meaning
Planet mars associated with the numerology of 9

The Humanist

The number 9 resonates with the the planet Mars.

The Numerology of 9: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 9 is imbued with immense strength and is often considered the most masculine of all numbers. It resonates with the energy of Mars, also known as Ares, the Roman God of war known for his physical and mental fortitude. The essence of the number 9 lies in its warrior-like qualities, including a strong will, determination, and independence. It approaches life with a focus on the essence of things, yet it also possesses a tender heart and sensitivity, reflecting Ares's dual nature as both a formidable warrior and a sensitive dancer.

Underneath its fierce exterior, the number 9 harbors a deeper, more humanitarian perspective on life. As the last of the single-digit numbers, it has absorbed the lessons of all preceding numbers, giving it an intuitive understanding of their unique challenges. This journey endows the number 9 with straightforward, uncompromising character traits, alongside immense compassion. The 9 prefers to empower others, teaching them to be self-reliant and resourceful, rather than providing temporary solutions. It champions justice and is driven to fight for a better world, often demonstrating natural leadership abilities.

The number 9 adopts a candid communication style, often sharing opinions and advice directly. This honesty can sometimes inadvertently hurt others' feelings or overwhelm more sensitive individuals. Despite its best intentions, the 9's directness can sometimes be received as harshness, leading to unintended consequences.

In its youth, the number 9 may have faced challenges, such as boundary violations, familial disharmony, or bullying, all of which served as tests of strength and resilience.

The 9 approaches work with focus and discipline, often achieving success later in life. It undergoes numerous trials to prove its worthiness of the "final glory" that its position as the last of the numbers symbolizes. Unlike the number 1, which is associated with beginnings and is often gifted with luck, the number 9 must demonstrate its merit to attain divine consciousness.

Though temperamental, the 9 typically maintains control over its emotions. However, when excessively provoked, it can erupt in a display of anger.

In relationships, the number 9 is protective, standing up for its loved ones with the same fervor it shows in other areas of life.

A balanced 9 harmoniously embodies both the warrior and the dancer, being in tune with its strength, body, and emotions. If overly aligned with its warrior aspect, it may neglect emotional and physical needs, leading to issues like overtraining or burnout. When the 9 energy is blocked, it can manifest as insecurity, confusion, depression, impracticality, and irresponsibility. Conversely, an excessive 9 energy can lead to oppressive, dictatorial, and militant behaviors, where its inherent sense of justice turns into self-righteousness, resembling an unpredictable volcano.

The life lesson for the number 9 is to embrace and acknowledge its feelings, tapping into its deep inner strength. In doing so, it achieves wisdom and balance.

The number 9 signifies divine completion and perfection. Encompassing all other numbers, it possesses a rich inner wisdom and access to higher consciousness. The 9 has both the fire and heart chakras activated, representing the ego and the soul, respectively.

Tarot card for 9 "The Hermit"

The number 9 corresponds to the 9th card in the Tarot, "The Hermit."

The tarot card shows an older man standing on the ice. In his right hand, he holds a lantern, and with his left hand, he holds a long stick.

The man is old, which symbolizes that the 9 is wise and has seen and tried it all.

The lantern symbolizes the light and the path - the 9 is here to show others the way. In the middle of the lamp, we see Solomon's star symbolizing wisdom. The stick represents the vertical masculine consciousness, which, like an axis, connects heaven and earth and which everything rotates around. The old man leans on this axis, which gives him his centering and power - and it is this inner power that makes the lantern shine.

The deep indigo color in the background symbolizes the transcendental consciousness of the 9. To see an in depth description of this tarot card please read this article:

Mars is 2/3 of the size of the Earth and has only approx. 60% of the gravitational field, so you can jump much higher on Mars than here on Earth.

Mars is the Roman god of war, which brings the final judgment and the end of times. Mars people are often warrior like and ready to conquer and subdue the world. They have the courage and strong will and, thanks to these qualities, achieve great success.

## Numerology 10 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 10

The Wheel of Fortune

The digits in the number 10 have the digit sum 1 which resonates with the Sun. 10 is often written as 10/1 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 10 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 10: Its Meaning and Significance

10 gives you a winner mentality, charisma, optimism, and cosmic intelligence. The number 10 resonates with the Sun (1) and the cosmos (0). 1 gives strength, power, winner mentality, initiative, and optimism. The 0 provides intuition, cosmic intelligence, and charisma.

10/1 is a number for luck, synchronicity, flow, winning mentality, harmony with cosmos, and an inner feeling of happiness. You are loved by all, spontaneous and playful, optimistic, open, and full of joy. You breeze through life because you feel protected by cosmos, which gives you intuition and luck. You gain success with the minimum effort because you always follow the synchronicities of life, which always show the direct and easy path.

Because of the number zero, it is called an "amplifier number," which increases the strength and intensity of the numbers located close to it in the chart. The positive numbers become even more positive while the unbalanced numbers worsen.

The number 10/1 is mainly known for its ability to "divide the waters," – meaning that there is a tendency to be loved or hated. The 10/1 can be so extreme in what it does and says that people either love it or hate it.

In imbalance, 10/1 is ungrounded, impatient, manic-depressive, and confused. Instead of being a number of divine order and success, the 10/1 makes you disorganized and have no success in life, giving you high blood pressure and stress. You are loved by some but really hated by most people. You become critical and over perfectionistic (like the number 1) and either on or off (1 or 0) - everything is full-on or nothing at all. In imbalance, 10/1 can be an austere, dry, protestant, and overly perfectionistic energy with too many principles often seen in missionaries, priests, and theologians (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

In balance, you are full of joy, optimism, and life smiles on you with good luck and synchronicities that always give you success and lead you up and higher.

On the card you see are four winged creatures in the corners of the card, representing the symbols of the four Evangelists (The Lion, the Ox, the Man and the Eagle).

These four Evangelists are also represented by the four fixed astrological signs: Taurus, Leo, Scorpio and Aquarius. The god Anubis rises with the wheel on the right side, while the snake-like Typhon descends on the left. On the wheel, alternating with the letters T-A-R-O are the Hebrew letters usually transliterated as YHWH (Yahweh - Hebrew for God). ## Numerology 19 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 19

The Heavenly Prince

The digits in the number 19 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 19 is often written as 19/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 19 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 19: Its Meaning and Significance

19 gives you luck, purity, and intuition. 1 and 9 add up to 10, which again adds up to 1, which is the number of the Sun. Like the Sun, the number 19 is full of energy and the spark of life. The Sun shines on its surroundings and leads others full of goodwill – the Sun gives a strong winner mentality, positive energy, and optimism.

The 19 is very optimistic and full of drive. With the number 19, you can experience remarkable synchronicities lucky coincidences and be carried forward by life.

There is a saying about 19/1: "Everything you touch turns into gold." This saying comes from the number 1 in 19 that initiates the project and the number 9 that makes it to the end. This makes the number 19/1 incredibly strong in business. Our statistics performed on the Forbes list from 2018 reveal that among the top 2000 billionaires, 19/1 features prominently in their charts. Especially as the big vocation number (which gives a great inner desire to be the best), and in the higher self chart, there is a high degree of 1s and 9s together subtly creating a 19/1 energy.

In imbalance, there can be a tendency to be narcissistic, spoiled, and want to remain forever young. You shirk responsibilities and prefer to stay in perpetual "party" mode. The imbalanced 19/1 energy can seem childish and unfocused on others and is not taken seriously. Your ideas can be ungrounded, and there could be so many of them that none materialize into anything. Others may also perceive your ideas as unrealistic, grandiose, and self-centered.

## Numerology 28 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 28

The Lion and Lamb

The digits in the number 28 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 28 is often written as 28/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 28 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 28: Its Meaning and Significance

28/1 gives you drive, enthusiasm, strength, winner mentality, and leadership skills.

You have the power to attain your goals and reach the top in whichever field you are in. You have the positive qualities from the 1 combined with great inner strength from the 8, 9, and 3 (in the diamond chart for 28/1 we also see the numbers 3 and 9). The number 2 gives you people skills and helps to soften the other numbers, which are quite hard. This provides 28/1 an incredibly business savvy because it understands how to turn people's needs into lucrative ideas.

In our research on the Forbes list from 2018, we discovered that the most common day of birth among the top 100 wealthiest people on earth was the 28th of the month. Billionaires such as Elon Musk, Bill Gates, Amancio Ortega, Carlos Slim, Samual Walton, Susanna Klatten, Serge Dassault, etc., are all billionaires born on the 28th of the month. Among the top 500 billionaires, the most common life path number was 28.

But it doesn't stop there. The 28/1 is not only good at amassing a fortune; our research also shows that the 28/1 is incredibly good at science. Together with the number 23, the number 28 shares the top placement for the most common day of birth in 3110 of the best scientists up through the ages. 28 also shares first place with 15 and 30 as the most common day of birth among the 654 Nobel prize winners in our statistics database. The number 28 is incredibly ambitious, and we see that it excels in many different fields with a high level of competition. Rather than saying that the 28/1 attracts wealth and creates billionaires, perhaps it is more correct to state that 28/1 thrives in any area with a lot at stake and a lot of competition and will naturally rise to the top in these fields.

In imbalance, there is a tendency that the goal justifies the means because it is so essential for 28/1 to reach the top (the 10/1 digit sum). Therefore, you must be careful not to create bad karma on your way, as the things you do can come back to you in less pleasant and unforeseen ways. This is due to the inner number 8 in 28/1, which stands for karma. 28/1 easily attracts lawsuits and litigation and is known as a "court case number." In strong imbalance, the number 28/1 resonates with the incredible greed that we, for example, saw in 2008 when a few wall street companies were instrumental in the collapse of the economy (note the subtle 28 embedded in the year 2008, which includes the 2 and 8). 28/1 in imbalance is a number of large financial American corporations.

## Numerology 37 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 37

Cleopatra

The digits in the number 37 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 37 is often written as 37/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 37 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 37: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 37 gives a "celebrity" vibe. It is about the courage to stand up for yourself, live your dreams, and be yourself precisely as you are without hiding or adhering to others' values. It is a powerful number that gives a magnetic charisma and an amazing love and sex life. It stands for the abundance of energy that flows into our lives. In Chaldean numerology, the number is also called the Royal Star of Taurus and refers to the constellation Taurus.

With this number, you are loved by all, enjoy great popularity, have a strong, seductive, and charismatic charisma, and easily make a lot of money. You live your life exactly as you are and stand up for yourself full of integrity. You have shine, charisma, and sex appeal and are successful in love, and money matters throughout life.

In the name chart, 37/1 is incredibly powerful and gives a high charisma and sex appeal. It is powerful as a name-value or as a big personality number (the consonants of the name added up). We often give it as a name value if the person lacks self-confidence and wants a better love life.

In imbalance, it gives endless drama, scandals, financial, and relationship problems. But this will only happen if it is surrounded by highly disharmonious numbers in the chart. 37/1 can be on the verge of a nervous breakdown due to an overactive life. It can be a troublemaker with a fallen rock star vibe inundated in scandals due to an outrageous lifestyle. It has problems with overspending and is restless and sexually frustrated. Instead of being a number that symbolizes a fantastic sex and love life, 37/1 in imbalance becomes a number where one misses deep and close relationships in one's life.

We often see the number 37/1 in the charts of celebrities who radiate an intense, sexual, and confident energy but often find themselves in all kinds of scandals.

When balanced, you have strong inner self-confidence, a fantastic sex, and love life, and you receive boundless respect and admiration.

## Numerology 46 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 46

The Apollon Politician

The digits in the number 46 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 46 is often written as 46/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 46 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 46: Its Meaning and Significance

46/1 is a powerful number that radiates beauty, strength, and authority. It is a vibration about improving and transforming the environment for the common good and breaking through in your career. This is frequently done through significant projects and philanthropic ideas.

There is something luminous around 46/1, like a "golden shine" in the aura that gives a great charisma and a magical glow to the person.

46/1 provides great gifts of speech and a deep understanding of rhetoric. People with 46/1 energy speak in a way where their intonation and rhythm back up their speech to the maximum.

46/1 is called "The Apollon Politician." When one mentions Apollon, the thoughts often go over to Dionysus as the opposition to Apollo. Dionysus was the god of the "wildness" and of passion. Apollon is therefore considered "presentable" or "civilized" compared to Dionysus. Apollon is the gilded son, the shiny "Magnificent hero" that wins over the subconscious forces through consciousness, art, morality, and the gift of speech. These properties are also seen in people who carry the 46/1 energy. The number 37/1 is sometimes called the "Cleopatra" number, but in reality, Cleopatra had the 46/1 energy since her last name, "Philopator," adds up to 46/1. Cleopatra was famous for her amazing charisma and political talent – hallmarks of the 46/1 energy.

Unbalanced, one tends to skip the necessary steps and radiate a superficial "glossy image" vibe. It becomes a glossy image with no real substance or soul where one is only concerned with looks, brand and image. Morals and virtues – for example family values are only used as a political “show” in order to get votes. The 46/1 can be submerged in chaos and scandals, desperately trying to keep it together, but inner ideals and mental images blur reality making it difficult to see fact from fiction.

In balance, you have excellent leadership skills, charisma, strength, and political sense.

## Numerology 55 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 55

The Media Number

The digits in the number 55 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 55 is often written as 55/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 55 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 55: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 55 provides valuable life lessons to mastering one's communication. The number 55 oozes charisma, optimism, gift of speech, charm, drive, and cares greatly for other people. It is driven by an almost fanatical inner drive to expand, win and reach the top. This number is born to be the leader and be the best in a field.

It is essential to always have seductive communication that entices people and gives a charismatic appeal.

An excellent example of a person who carries this energy is Arnold Schwarzenegger. Schwarzenegger gives 55/1. He came from a small city in Austria and has achieved great success in bodybuilding, acting, and politics.

In imbalance, it's a lot about going for what you want, no matter the means, and that you can be so ambitious that you resort to unethical methods. The many 5's can also result in a scattered mind, and you could experience trouble focusing. Another challenge is procrastination and getting lost in "small pleasures" - small pastimes that give short-term pleasure/income but that don't serve you in the long run.

In balance, it is about having a considerable radiance and charisma that makes you good with the media and helps you achieve great success.

Explore more about the numerology of 55 and its powerful implications in our online program "Chaldean Numerology for Beginners." Uncover every aspect of this archetype through ™, detailed statistics, Tarot, Tree of life, and other numerological interpretations. Click here to learn more about Chaldean Numerology for Beginners and how you can start your journey towards deeper knowledge and understanding.

The 55 in balance is:

Excellent with different kinds of media

A seductive communicator that entices people

Has a spellbinding charisma and is very charming

Creating its own reality and rising to the top of any career

Making a cohesive field around it, that lifts the energies of all around

The 55 unbalanced is

Easily distracted by the sensual pleasures in life

Too much talk and too little action

Trapped in materialism and loses sight of the spiritual aspects

Feeling like a victim of the neverending temptations of the world

Loved or hated – others are jealous or cannot stand the perceived narcissism

The number 55 is a master number

The number 55 is a master number meaning that the first digit is the same as the last digit. This means that the "inner lessons" (dictated by the inner digit) are the same as the "outer lessons" (dictated by the outer digit). So how we manage our inner life is reflected in the outer life.

Karma

Master numbers in our charts are the carriers of "karma" - if you didn't understand the lessons represented by a certain number in your previous incarnations, you get double up in this life - either in your birthday numbers or in your names. So having master numbers can feel "heavy" or "difficult."

But this karma can also be burned quickly if you start performing the right actions. Since the inner and outer digits are the same, there is a tendency that whatever you think becomes a reality - which is bad news if your inner world is full of pessimism and dark thoughts. But once you start aligning your inner world with the power of the number in its balanced form, the opposite also holds - you will begin manifesting a life of beauty and abundance.

Master your thoughts

One of the first lessons, when you have a master number in your charts (for example, a birthday number or essence number), is that you have to start mastering your thoughts and what vibrational frequencies you send into the world. What you think and focus upon should revolve around the balanced aspects of the inner digit (in this case, the number 5) and should be expressed out in the world through the digit sum of the number - in this case, 10.

The lessons of 55

Since this is the number 55 it means that your inner desire to communicate and bring people together is best channeled into the outer world by finding a single idea you can focus on and bringing this idea into fruition. This idea will find its greatest fruition if it involves taking you upwards in the levels of society. There is a tendency with this number to become lost in the world of senses, sensuality and materialism. The temptation is that of hedonism - so beware of this and don't lose sight of the higher goal!

## Numerology 64 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 64

The Mayor

The digits in the number 64 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 64 is often written as 64/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 64 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 64: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 64/1 has great perfectionism and a sense of harmony and balance. People with this number in their charts have a kind of uniqueness, beauty, and perfection in their aura where they both are beautiful (from the number 6), unique (from the number 4), and luminous (from the number 1). Many famous artists have this number in their charts.

Although 64/1 may not be beautiful in the traditional sense, it typically has a clean, well-trimmed, well-groomed appearance, which comes from both the exterior and an inner state of clarity and purity.

The 64/1 sense of harmony can lead to extreme inner discomfort if the surroundings are untidy, immoral, or not live up to the highest standard. Many people with 64/1 in their charts are very politically active and make good "mayors" because they are interested in the community. The idea is that if everybody lives up to their responsibilities and are good citizens, then the world will be a much better place.

In imbalance, one can appear a little dry and overly moralizing. There can be too many principles where 64/1 expects that everybody does things in specific ways. It can be critical to the extreme where nothing ever is good enough. 64/1 can also be quietly pompous – "I know better than you." Sometimes 64/1 can also be a little bit dull – it can rant on indefinitely with statistics and numbers.

In balance, 64/1 gives the energy of being a wise person with big ideas that always considers the common good.

64/1 shares many of the same aspects as 46/1. We recommend you read about 46/1 to understand 64/1 on a more profound level.

## Numerology 73 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 73

The Secret Millionaire

The digits in the number 73 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 73 is often written as 73/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 73 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 73: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 73 is a vibration about creating ingenious and innovative things to create a better world, leading to great wealth without being noticed. The 73/1 likes this because it doesn't desire to take center stage. You have a tremendous pioneer spirit and the ability to lead and become wealthy.

73/1 is a sister vibration of 37/1, but it's a lot more discrete energy than the 37/1. Where 37/1 has almost a "rock star"-like extroversion where you stand out full of self-confidence and do things the way you want to (without overthinking), then 73/1 uses the controversial "Rock star" energy to put into its creations, and has no problem remaining in the background.

73/1 also wants greatness and success like 37/1 but does things in a more subtle, thoughtful, and spiritual way because we here have the number 7 facing the outer world instead of the number 3 in 37/1. Where the number 3 takes up a lot of space and attracts attention, then the number 7 facing outwards is more empathetic, uses its intuition, and senses the surroundings to get the things it wants. 73/1 is also a brilliant number for writers. In our statistics, we see a lot of authors, poets, and novelists who have 73/1 figure in prominent places in their charts.

In imbalance, there can be fatigue about being with other people and an excessive need for privacy. 73/1 can be tired of the outer obligations and longs for a place where the grass is greener. It can also suffer from spotlight allergy, where it feels that everybody knows the slightest details of its life. It unwillingly helps others still if it hates it and would prefer to be left in peace. It becomes like a bubbling volcano that never erupts - constantly slightly annoyed. It sees others as spiritually and mentally weak but only helps them so that it can get paid. Over time it develops a very cynical energy.

In balance, you radiate beauty, strength and are seen as a genius.

## Numerology 82 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 82

The Religious Leader

The digits in the number 82 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 82 is often written as 82/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 82 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 82: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 82/1 vibration gives a strong winning mentality, and many talents used to lift a community. 82/1 uses its immense strength to shine and reach the top in administration, politics, and religion. There is an ability to attract a large following in the number, and in this way, you become almost a form of "religious leader."

The sister vibration of 82/1 is 28/1, which is a number that brings enormous success. Statistics show that 28/1 is not only one of the strongest numbers when it comes to money (seen on the Forbes list, among others) - it is also a number that frequently appears in all kinds of competitive disciplines. For example, we see that many athletes have 28/1 among their numbers. However, 82/1 is more focused on success through religion and power, whereas 28/1 is more focused on financial success or success in sports. Like 28/1, 82/1 is a number that potentially attracts a lot of court cases and legal issues.

In imbalance, 82/1 makes you hot-tempered, lack consideration, do a lot of hard work without results and attract lawsuits and litigation. 82/1 can divide the waters and be loved or hated due to its perceived bossiness. It can be self-centered, unempathetic, and lack interest in other people who it sees as inferior. It can be fanatic in its desire to reach the top of society. Like its sister vibration 28/1, it has a kind of false modesty that attracts lawsuits because it humbly promises something in one moment and then conveniently forgets about it in the next.

In balance, you are talented, expansive, and a strong leader who lifts the group.

If the number 82/1 appears in the year row charts, it is usually an indication of being able to lead others in this period as you will be seen as a religious/spiritual leader. When Donald Trump lost the election to Biden, he had 82/1 in his year rows. There were lawsuits attacking the election's legitimacy (the 82/1 resonance with litigation), and Trump was elevated to the role of a kind of Qanon religious savior figure by his followers.

Egoistic, unempathetic, and lacks interest in other people

Sees other people as inferior and has no problem bossing them around

False modesty which attracts lawsuits

## Numerology 91 Meaning
Planet sun associated with the numerology of 91

Greatness

The digits in the number 91 add up to 10 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 1 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Sun. 91 is often written as 91/1 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 91 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 10 here and the primary digit sum 1 here.

The Numerology of 91: Its Meaning and Significance

91/1 is a magnificent and expansive vibration that wants to conquer the world with new ideas. Every number in the 90s is a master of the inner number. 91 is, therefore, a master of the ego (1).

91/1 thinks and dreams big. It has significant arm movements. It sees everything in the light of greatness. You can see yourself on stage in front of thousands of people. You see yourself as victorious and great. It desires enormous success and has great inner inspiration, good leadership skills, and oratory skills. Before the ego is mastered and transcended, there is a tendency in 91/1 to use the power of the ego to instill fear and respect in others.

In imbalance, therefore, there can be strong egotism, narcissistic tendencies, and delusions of grandeur, unconsciously feeling small, and as a defense mechanism developing an exaggerated belief in one's abilities, but without any basis in reality. This tendency can make the 91/1 very obnoxious. There can be an extreme "my way or the highway" worldview. There can also be manic-depressive tendencies where 91/1 is flying high in one moment with high energy and visions of grandeur and then crashes in the next moment feeling completely crushed. 91/1 can have difficulty listening and taking in feedback and have an overly logical and robotic approach to emotions.

In balance, the 91/1 transcends the ego and becomes larger than life itself. It becomes an unstoppable force that creates a significant change in the world.

## Numerology 11 Meaning
Planet moon associated with the numerology of 11

The Teamplayer Empathic

The digits in the number 11 have the digit sum 2 which resonates with the Moon. 11 is often written as 11/2 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 11 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 2 here.

The Numerology of 11: Its Meaning and Significance

11/2 gives you empathy, kindness, and community-mindedness. 1 and 1 add up to 2, the number of the moon – and like the moon, the number 11 is soft, receptive, sensual, empathic, and cares for others.

At the same time, 11/2 has great spiritual potential with clairvoyant and telepathic gifts. As a number 11, you are very receptive – to such a degree that you can quickly feel other people at a profound level – it is almost as if you can hear what they are thinking inside your mind. If fully developed, this skill can lead to a high level of clairvoyance where you can read other people and communicate non verbally.

The two number 1s in 11/2 create a personality that continuously weighs two different options against each other. For example, we find many lawyers and judges born on the 11th of the month as these jobs need you to weigh different options and opposing viewpoints against each other (Numerologist PRO Statistics). This corresponds beautifully with the Tarot Card for 11/2 called "justice."

In imbalance, the number 11 makes you unable to set healthy boundaries. People go over your limits because you have pleaser tendencies and are afraid of conflict. You overhelp and put others in front of yourself – but become resentful. You feel vulnerable and unprotected. You become so sensitive that disharmonious energies in the surroundings can give you headaches and migraines. Imbalanced 11/2 also gives indecision - you can never decide anything and wind up procrastinating. There is also a tendency to soothe emotions with sugar, food, shopping, entertainment, etc.

The number 11/2 is not good in the name chart as it weakens all the adjacent numbers, bringing forth their imbalanced aspects.

In balance, the 11/2 becomes a perfect number for teambuilding and lifting groups of people up. The inner winner mentality (from the inner 1) goes together with the outer winner mentality (1) to lift the group (symbolized by the digit sum 2) and unite others around a higher purpose. You become selfless, loving, helpful, and highly empathetic. You are honor-bound and expect it from others also.

Tarot card for 11 "Justice"

The tarot card for number 11 is called “Justice”. The figure on the card holds a scale made of gold in their left hand, symbolizing a balanced decision.

On the surface level the card signifies justice, equity, rightness, making sure that the law is upheld etc.

At a deeper level it symbolizes how 11/2 can balance its inner and outer energies in order to find inner peace. To see an in depth description of this tarot card please read this article:

IN DEPTH WITH TAROT CARD 11

The 11 in balance is:

A team player that lifts the whole community

Selfless, Loving, helpful and highly empathetic

Honor bound and expects it from others also

Uniting others around a higher purpose

Feeling all other people in the room and taking on their emotions

The 11 unbalanced is

A people pleaser who is afraid of conflict

Overhelping and putting others in front of itself – but is resentful

Indecisive - cannot make decisions and procrastinates

Vulnerable, unprotected and taken advantage of by others

Soothing difficult emotions with sugar, food, shopping, etc.

The number 11 is a master number

The number 11 is a master number meaning that the first digit is the same as the last digit. This means that the "inner lessons" (dictated by the inner digit) are the same as the "outer lessons" (dictated by the outer digit). So how we manage our inner life is reflected in the outer life.

One of the first lessons, when you have a master number in your charts (for example, a birthday number or essence number), is that you have to start mastering your thoughts and what vibrational frequencies you send into the world. What you think and focus upon should revolve around the balanced aspects of the inner digit (in this case, the number 1) and should be expressed out in the world through the digit sum of the number - in this case, 2.

The lessons of 11

Since this is the number 11, it means that you should express your individuality and winning mentality (1) in the outer world through the number 2 - which stands for emotional connection, groups of people, and teams. Master number 11 finds its true calling when it uses its inner fire (1) to lift the energy of a group (2). If you focus on the negative aspects of the number 1 energy - which is individuality and egotism, while also ignoring the collective energy in the number 2, you will attract the imbalanced elements of the number 1 in your outer world - meaning you will attract people into your life who only think of themselves and are selfish.

NUMBER 11 keywords for inspiration

Empathy, receptive, thoughtful, helpful, soft, gentle, loves harmony and peace, sensitive, sensual, enjoys life, creative, higher humanitarian goals, unselfish, goes for win-win situations, lifts up a team by making the individuals focus on a higher common goals, feels all other people in the room, high level of empathy, spiritual teacher, dances together with the divine, honour bound, expects the highest from others.

The imbalanced aspects, traits and adjectives of number 11 are:

Afraid of conflict, puts others in front of itself – but is resentful, indecisive, inner division, insecurity, is taken advantage of, soothes the emotions (with over eating, sugar, smoking, alcohol, shopping etc.), inner emptiness feeling, feels unprotected, afraid to be unpopular, feels overseen, victimization feeling.

## Numerology 20 Meaning
Planet moon associated with the numerology of 20

Passion

The digits in the number 20 have the digit sum 2 which resonates with the Moon. 20 is often written as 20/2 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 20 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 2 here.

The Numerology of 20: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 20 is all about following your passion in life and never thinking about money since it will always be there anyway. The fundamental state of number 20 is to feel protected and always be surrounded by gentle, positive angelic energies and a deep inner blissful peace.

With the number 20, you naturally inspire others and have a strong intuition that helps you follow your path in life. You have a tremendous amount of joy and passion in everything that you do. Before taking on any project, it is essential that it feels right and makes sense to you on all levels of your being.

In all that you do, you have to be in touch with your emotions, and in this way, you can make a profound and positive change in the world through your unique contribution.

The number 20/2 gives a remarkable ability to tap into the collective unconscious (the number 2 resonates with the moon and the subconscious mind). The 0 further boosts this ability to tap into the collective unconscious. This makes people born on the 20th of the month extremely strong in knowing what to communicate to people. In our statistics on 6594 politicians and political leaders through history, we find that the most common day of birth is the 20th of the month. In our statistics on 4100 artists, the 20th is the second most common day of birth.

Unbalanced, you have a hard time finding what you really should be doing in life and having a hard time finding your passion. You are often ungrounded and impatient with your projects and other people. You can be sad that you are not contributing to the world, and finding your passion feels like a big insurmountable challenge. There is a tendency in imbalance to constantly change your passions and never find one thing you can stick with. Alternatively, you can be overpassioned, manic, and too intense with your beliefs. Your plans fail with sadness and melancholy as a result. 20/2 can also be fanatic with its ideals of creating a better world and act like an oracle with a patent on the truth.

In balance, you are in touch with your emotions, passions, and the collective unconscious, and in this way, you can make a profound and positive change in the world through your unique contribution to the world.

## Numerology 29 Meaning
Planet moon associated with the numerology of 29

The Coach

The digits in the number 29 add up to 11 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 2 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Moon. 29 is often written as 29/2 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 29 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 11 here and the primary digit sum 2 here.

The Numerology of 29: Its Meaning and Significance

29/2 gives a great empathy and the ability to guide and coach other people. 2 is the number of the moon, and like the moon, the number 29 is soft, receptive, sensual, empathic, and cares for those around it. The number 29 is a person full of energy – the mixture of 2 and 9 (water and fire) creates a considerable amount of electrical energy that surges through the nervous system of the person under the influence of 29/2. 29/2 gives a big heart and a wide range of emotions. You're also creative, fun, and very entertaining (from the 2 and digit sum 11/2).

As a number 29, it will come naturally to guide and coach other people. The outer number 2 is empathic, and the inner number 9 has an overview, an ability to synthesize problems and give guidance. So you intuitively feel where the other person is with the number 2, and you make a synthesis with number 9, finding the best way to help that person take action and solve their problems.

Among a few examples of people who use their empathy to feel into others is TV interviewer Oprah Winfrey and life coach Anthony Robbins. Both are born on the 29th of the month; both have a high level of energy and an ability to empathize with others, feel where they are, and lead them forward.

Our research carried out on 16185 entertainers (actors, tv show hosts, radio hosts, interviewers, etc.) found that the most common life path number is 29/2. On our research carried out on 11555 musicians (both rhythmic and classical), the most common life path number is also 29/2. This shows the enormous ability that 29/2 has both in relating to others but also in being able to entertain.

In imbalance, 29/2 is a number of naivety, where you get hurt and let down in love affairs and do too much for other people who show you no gratitude. You get treated like a doormat and get nothing in return, which leads to a lot of inner anger and frustration (inner 9 imbalanced). 29/2 in imbalance is also a number that attracts court cases like 28/1. Where 28/1 attracts law cases due to questionable ethics, 29/2 attracts lawsuits out of naivety and being duped by others who see the opportunity to win quick money.

## Numerology 38 Meaning
Planet moon associated with the numerology of 38

The Peacemaker

The digits in the number 38 add up to 11 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 2 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Moon. 38 is often written as 38/2 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 38 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 11 here and the primary digit sum 2 here.

The Numerology of 38: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 38 has a unique ability to create harmony in the surroundings, is a strong messenger of love and peace, and possesses great power to convince others. In the higher self chart, the number indicates a gentle and influential person who uniquely unites gentleness and strength. You will like to help others and communicate your ideas to the world. You can be a bit of a world savior (11/2) who has great strength (8) and a desire for success (3).

The 38 vibration provides a good understanding of energies on the physical and etheric (energetic) planes, and one can be talented within things such as Fengshui, healing via meridians, acupuncture, etc.

The "coach" (number 29/2) is about your advice to guide others emotionally and personally. With 38/2, you are another kind of adviser. The number may attract high-ranking people who come to you for advice and guidance, both strategically and morally, about creating peace and harmony on a more significant level, e.g., in society or politically.

When imbalanced, there is a tendency to feel unappreciated, not heard, and overlooked. There can also be an extreme preoccupation with exploring the balances of power in relationships, which in some instances, can lead to sadomasochistic tendencies.

When balanced, the number 38 can harmonize the energies in the surroundings through an acute sense of awareness that allows it to feel the energy flow, energy spheres, and meridians.

## Numerology 47 Meaning
Planet moon associated with the numerology of 47

The Sensitive Healer

The digits in the number 47 add up to 11 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 2 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the Moon. 47 is often written as 47/2 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 47 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 11 here and the primary digit sum 2 here.

The Numerology of 47: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 47/2 is a vibration about sensitivity, imagination, dreams, and hopes. It has a great talent in art and also possesses healing abilities.

47/2 can be rebellious in a quiet sort of way. It doesn't want to fit into society in the traditional roles. The number is often seen in the charts of artists, poets, and thinkers who wish to go their own ways. We have here the archetype of the "misunderstood and sensitive loner."

47/2 is one of the most melancholy numbers. The number 4 is often unhappy and dreams of another or better place. The number 7 may be distant and live in a self-made parallel universe. 11/2 is a sensitive number that also often longs for the princess or prince of his/her dreams. So in 47/2, we find the 3 most longing and melancholic numbers wrapped up into a single number!

In imbalance, 47/2 gives a desire to escape from the world, and one might prefer books, nature, or computers rather than human company. There can also be a tendency to melancholy and depression.

In balance, it's a number that stimulates artistic and creative abilities, and it gives an ability to create something unique that you give to the world.
