## Numerology 15 Meaning
Planet venus associated with the numerology of 15

The Feminine Force

The digits in the number 15 have the digit sum 6 which resonates with the planet Venus. 15 is often written as 15/6 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 15 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 6 here.

The Numerology of 15: Its Meaning and Significance

15 gives you considerable charisma, shine, and sensuality. 1 and 5 add up to 6, the number of Venus, the planet of sensuality, beauty, harmony, and uplifted femininity. The 15/6 variant of the number 6 is the most feminine and mysterious of all the numbers – and a potentially tricky number to master. The number contains tremendous erotic power, a sense of pleasure, and sensuality. There is also a powerful occult element to 15/6, which can put you in contact with parallel worlds, spirits, ghosts, entities, etc.

15/6 gives an aesthetic sense, artistic talent, and an ability to shine light into other people's darkness and heal others through art and beauty. The mere presence of a person with a healthy 15 energy exudes such beautiful energies that it helps people heal physical and emotional trauma.

15/6 gives beauty, good artistic, and people skills. Many people born on the 15th are actors, artists, graphic artists, and models (Numerologist PRO Statistics). And this becomes especially apparent when scrolling down the list of celebrities born on the 15th of the month. Most of them exude a special kind of sensual charisma characteristic of the number 15/6 (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

In imbalance, the 15/6 gives manipulation, gossip, drama, jealousy, bickering, etc. It strongly influences other people, especially those physically present around it. This can lead to manipulating others without consciously wanting to because you have a very strong power. You can get in a terrible mood over the most minor things, which can feel incredibly big, such as a negative comment from someone that makes you spill a glass of water or miss the bus. There is a tendency for drama in relationships, losing one's relationships, and not letting go. 15/6, when placed close to disharmonious numbers in the name chart, can lead to all sorts of mysterious illnesses. 15/6 in imbalance can also lead to evil and occult practices. It can be manipulative, demonic, evil, occult, and psychopathic, incessantly plotting and scheming – and enjoying seeing it go badly for others. In imbalance, it is the most dramatic of all numbers, with substantial mood swings that affect others. It can be a player/charmeur, using charm for own pleasure and gain. 15/6 is constantly getting under your skin, remembering details it can use against you, and catching you off guard.

In balance, the 15/6 is charming, mysterious, and has a great power of attraction on others. It is the most sensual number, with a loving, enticing, and alluring charisma. It shines a light into the darkness of others through its presence. It can be very emotionally intelligent, making it an excellent actor because it understands emotion. It can be delightful and feminine with an awakened aesthetic sense making it a number for amazing art and design.

## Numerology 24 Meaning
Planet venus associated with the numerology of 24

The Queen

The digits in the number 24 have the digit sum 6 which resonates with the planet Venus. 24 is often written as 24/6 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 24 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 6 here.

The Numerology of 24: Its Meaning and Significance

24 gives you beauty, harmony, charisma, and the ability to gather people around you and create a vibration of togetherness based on a feeling of being together in a family.

It is called a queen number because it combines beauty and harmony with a high inner level of consciousness and strength, just as required of a queen. In the essence of the diamond chart for 24/6, we see the number 9, a masculine number that symbolizes the consciousness principle. 24/6 unites masculine and feminine and becomes a high vibrating and soft energy, which at the same time holds a strong inner core of consciousness that strives upwards towards something more elevated.

24/6 is also known for helping you attract help from people in the higher echelons of society. This, together with the excellent network abilities, also makes the 24/6 a very strong number in business.

24/6 is a number of art and aesthetics and is carried by many artists and designers. In our statistics performed on 4100 artists, we see that the 24th is the most common day of birth. It is also the leading day of birth among Architects. 24/6 is also a number of patriotism since it also has a great love of community and traditions. In this regard, it is interesting that our statistics performed on 2596 famous military personalities up through history reveal that most were born on the 24th. The patriotic drive in the number 24 makes many born on the 24th interested in serving in the military.

When the 24/6 energy is unbalanced, you can experience losing out in your love relationships. You feel alone with everything and that there is no love and support. You feel that you deserve more respect and admiration than you get, and it feels as if the inner queen does not come forth and is not getting seen. You don't get help from anybody, and you feel alone with everything. There can be economic problems and overspending. 24/6 can be manipulative, dramatic, stubborn, angry, controlling, and dominating in close relationships.

In balance, the number 24/6 gives a fantastic sense of harmony and beauty. It makes you popular in your family and community while also giving you a solid and healthy sense of patriotism.

## Numerology 33 Meaning
Planet venus associated with the numerology of 33

Master Teacher

The digits in the number 33 have the digit sum 6 which resonates with the planet Venus. 33 is often written as 33/6 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 33 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 6 here.

The Numerology of 33: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 33 provides inner strength and can transform lower emotions into a higher, more loving, and compassionate energy. There is an inner core of wisdom and strong principles.

The 33/6 vibration teaches you to lift your own vibration through an interior "Practice" where you are your own spiritual teacher. Through a strong inner fire and self-discipline, combined with the desire to live in love and compassion, you burn the impurities up in the heart and the mind and transform lower tendencies, desires, and low vibrating emotions to high vibrating emotions and states.

The number 33 is greatly coveted in many masonic systems (33 hierarchies or levels). It lies inherent in the psychology of the number 33 that the world is split up into different levels where you have to compete for your way to the top level – and that the higher levels are allowed to exploit and take advantage of the lower levels.

In this world view, the few at the top are entitled to lead the masses. In this symbolism, we find many references to the pyramid, a symbol for this hierarchical structure. If we think of 32 as the absolute money number, then 33 is the level above that – where power is focused, and the power resides just above the economy (symbolized by 32). This is why we have expressions such as "to find who has the power then follow the money."

The number 33 wants to control the very fabric of power and economy in society. This is why the number 33 holds such importance for global power and banking cliches. We find the number 33 in the charts of many ambitious politicians. In these cases, it is usually subtly represented in the chart as, for example, people born on a 3 day (the 3rd, 12th, 21st, or 30th of a month) and with a 3 life path number (12,21,30,39) subtly creates the 33/6 energy.

Imbalanced, there is a tendency for harshness, competition, and comparing oneself to others. 33/6 can be stressed because it always has to defend its territory and achievements. There is also a tendency to be dry, old-fashioned, and conservative, and a considerable stubbornness where you are unwilling to learn or budge from your opinions. The feminine energy (the number 6) can be blocked and repressed by the masculine energy (the two number 3s). Seeing life as a pyramid of power where the top has to be reached can also block access to the soul because everything is measured in status, power, achievement, and the ability to ascend up through the levels of society and politics.

In balance, 33 gives an ability to overcome lower tendencies to become a "master teacher" - a mighty pillar of light and love. It is a master number (double 3).

The 33 in balance is:

Creating wealth and power to protect the family

Focused on creating a better world and lifting humanity to a new level

A master teacher that transforms negative inner tendencies to love

Having the authority and strength to make the world a better place

Advancing through the ranks and degrees to the pinnacles of success

The 33 unbalanced is

Overly competitive and comparing itself with others

Stressed because it has to defend its territory and achievements

Dry and boring - the feminine energy is blocked and repressed

Oldfashioned, conservative and not willing to learn or budge

Seeing life as a pyramid of power where the top has to be reached

The number 33 is a master number

The number 33 is a master number meaning that the first digit is the same as the last digit. This means that the "inner lessons" (dictated by the inner digit) are the same as the "outer lessons" (dictated by the outer digit). So how we manage our inner life is reflected in the outer life.

One of the first lessons, when you have a master number in your charts (for example, a birthday number or essence number), is that you have to start mastering your thoughts and what vibrational frequencies you send into the world. What you think and focus upon should revolve around the balanced aspects of the inner digit (in this case, the number 3) and should be expressed out in the world through the digit sum of the number - in this case, 6.

The lessons of 33

Since this is the number 33, it means that your intellectual capacities (the number 3) should be expressed in the outer world through the number 6 - which stands for harmony and beauty.

The imbalanced number 3 tends to strategize about things and also to compete with others constantly. This continual mentalizing of the world means that if you have 33 imbalanced in your charts, you will attract a lot of endless strategizing and hostile competition from other people.

Instead, you should awaken your heart and channel your mental energies into creating things of beauty and which bring cohesion (6). It is for this reason that the awakened number 33 is called "The master teacher."

## Numerology 42 Meaning
Planet venus associated with the numerology of 42

Heal The World

The digits in the number 42 have the digit sum 6 which resonates with the planet Venus. 42 is often written as 42/6 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 42 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 6 here.

The Numerology of 42: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 42/6 is all about healing, love, and creating positive change in the world. Like in 24/6 and 33/6, there is a great desire to make the world more harmonious. In 42/6, this is even more visible to others, and thus it is a big part of one's "brand" that one wants to create positive change.

The vibration is very similar to 24/6 but has more difficulty relating to other people personally. It can easily be perceived as a bit strange (4 and 10/1 in the diamond chart) but is usually seen as a unique and loving person. 42/6 gives excellent transforming and healing abilities, which others can benefit from.

In imbalance, 42/6 can easily get misunderstood and be considered odd and a bit fanatical with its ideas. It can stand alone with its high ideals that nobody gets. It can be overly politically correct and forget the needs of others. There can be a tendency to put up a facade where it always is showing 'how good and morally superior' it is. It keeps a suffocating eye on others to ensure that they live up to the ideals, making it loved or hated due to its over idealism. In strong imbalance, it can give a dictatorial energy where it becomes the only one with the right ideas to lead the world forward.

When balanced, the 42/6 brings healing, idealism, love, and power to change the world in a unique way.

Planet venus associated with the numerology of 51

Advancement

The digits in the number 51 have the digit sum 6 which resonates with the planet Venus. 51 is often written as 51/6 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 51 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 6 here.

The Numerology of 51: Its Meaning and Significance

The central theme in the number 51 vibration is about advancing to new heights through its charismatic, strategic, and social capabilities. It can be a belligerent vibration where the goal justifies the means, and it is not generally used in the name chart. 51/6 is associated with the military and especially the navy.

There may be a tendency to interfere and snag/gossip into other peoples affairs, which is used to get to the top. In international politics this translates to intelligence agencies and spying. The more you know about the social landscape, the quicker you can progress – especially if you potentially could blackmail somebody.

In the traditional Chaldean numerology sources, it is also described that the number 51, with its rapid and slightly belligerent advancement, can result in quite a few enemies. In part, the employees can get angry, but the competitors (e.g., in business) may also become angry - or jealous of your success. They may be hiding their ill will but secretly be waiting and lurking in the shadows until they have the opportunity to take revenge and rejoice in watching the number 51/6 go down. The numerologist Cheiro describes the number 51 as a number that can attract assassinations. Interestingly, JFK had the numbers 51/6 in his numerological year rows when he was assassinated. It also happened when he was experiencing a significant advancement both in terms of his politics and in terms of his enormous popularity in the general population. This growing popularity was probably too much for specific forces who wanted him taken out of the way.

The Numerology of 69: Its Meaning and Significance

It works from behind the scenes and can use female guile, charm, and charisma to achieve its desires - but with a more profound wisdom that underlies everything it does so that harmony and balance are ensured.

In 69/6, the central theme is the meeting between the masculine consciousness and the feminine energy. It can include anything from flirting, to sexuality, to the most exalted dance between the masculine and the feminine. But it can also symbolize the great drama that can unfold between men and women when erotic energy is involved.

In imbalance, there is drama, misunderstandings, manipulation, jealousy, quarrels, gossip, back-stabbing, and a tendency to use one's powers selfishly. When 69/6 is unbalanced, we find drama at its worst, where charm and erotic appeal are used in a highly manipulative way. 69/6 can destroy the relationships of others through unconscious (or conscious) drama. There may also be a tendency to exploit others emotionally and sexually. There can also be materialistic tendencies, greed, and selfishness.

In balance, there is a vast potential of love, spirituality, energy, healing, and a high level of consciousness.

## Numerology 96 Meaning
Planet venus associated with the numerology of 96

Cassanova

The digits in the number 96 add up to 15 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 6 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the planet Venus. 96 is often written as 96/6 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 96 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 15 here and the primary digit sum 6 here.

The Numerology of 96: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 96 completely controls the interaction between masculine (9) and feminine (6) energy. You master your emotions, charm, and charisma and, at the same time, have a high awareness of yourself and others.

96/6 gives a strong flamboyance, appeal, and attractiveness, making it charming and attractive to the opposite sex. The number 96/6 is called "Cassanova," the intense lover whose life purpose is to fully explore the world of sensuality and sexuality. Casanova's numerological psychic self chart is full of the numbers 15/6 and 9, which gave him the intense drive to explore sexuality in every form.

96/6 also gives a great appreciation of art, and many who have 96/6 in their charts are skilled artists and musicians. We also see a lot of the combination of 9 and 15/6 in the charts of fine dining cooks and pastry chefs (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

In imbalance, one can use this power selfishly. 96/6 can be a Cassanova in a bad way, consciously manipulating the opposite sex. There can also be a lot of drama regarding love relations and all the emotions that come with that: jealousy, resentment, arguments, misunderstandings, etc. 96/6 can be overly flamboyant and has a penchant for lying. It can also be excessively focused on clothes, shoes, perfumes, status objects, etc. There can be a tendency to always be seeking pleasure, and it gambles its money away, spending its life on idle pastimes.

In balance, the number is full of love and awareness and wishes to achieve a state of divine love where masculine and feminine polarities are balanced.

## Numerology 16 Meaning
Planet neptune associated with the numerology of 16

The Tower

The digits in the number 16 have the digit sum 7 which resonates with the planet Neptune. 16 is often written as 16/7 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 16 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 7 here.

The Numerology of 16: Its Meaning and Significance

The specific number 16 variant of number 7 is the most transcendent, wise, and profound aspect of the number 7 – it is the variant that easiest can melt into the void and enter deep states of Zen-like meditation.

The number 16 is a karmic number. This means that there could potentially be terrifying life lessons following the 16. The most important experience is the lesson of transcendence – meaning that you need to learn at a deep level that everything in this life is transient and that everything will die, perish, get consumed by existence, and return to nothingness. This aspect can lead to extreme inner anxiety, making the number 16 difficult to master.

The number 16/7 is always imbalanced in the name chart, where it gives sorrow, grief, and fear in the area of life that it is placed in. 16/7 can make you paralyzed by anxiety due to the previous loss of loved ones. This fear makes you unable to connect to others, and you can feel profoundly lonely. You are afraid of being annihilated, fearful of heights, and full of phobias. You always follow the rules out of fear, becoming a sheep in the herd and easy to manipulate with fear tactics. There is an inner feeling of always standing and observing people and life itself from the outside, unable to participate.

We desire to live a life of abundance and plenitude, and life is not meant to be a constant struggle with fear and anxiety. If you have 16/7 in your name chart and you have found this page, perhaps it means that your soul has led you here through the process of resonance, and you are ready to let go of the karmic lessons of pain and suffering. If this is the case, we strongly recommend you consult with a name change numerologist to rectify your names and give your life a new and positive meaning and direction.

In balance, the 16/7 becomes wise, spiritual, and detached. But it can only be balanced in the natal chart, where it is a potential that can swing to its balanced or imbalanced sides depending on the numbers in the name chart. The balanced 16/7 is observant, skilled, and mentally awakened, both scientific and philosophical - seeing beyond the surface. It is the most transcendent number - giving an inner joy beyond this world. It can dive into the depths of the soul/psyche and see deep patterns. It learns profound lessons of life by going through its fears and transcending them.

The Numerology of 25: Its Meaning and Significance

25 gives you a depth of personality, a spiritual outlook on life, artistic talent, and great wisdom that has been gained through experience.

You easily attract an international career (the number 7) - or a life with a lot of travel, vacations, or you, for example, follow your spouse to a new country. You are very adventurous and dare to follow your heart and live out your passions.

25/7 is sometimes called "The spiritual workshop number" because, in a spiritual workshop, you often use sensuality (related to the 2) your senses (related to the 2 and 5) to communicate with others (related to 5 energy), to create a higher spiritual understanding (related to 7 energy). In this way, the 2 and 5 can combine their forces in spiritual workshops to develop themselves.

The number 25/7 also gives excellent writing skills because writing allows you to communicate (the number 5) and thereby extract higher spiritual conclusions (the number 7). Our research performed on 3948 writers reveals that the most common day of birth is the 25th. In our study of 16185 entertainers, the 25th featured as the second most common day of birth (after the 17th), showing that the number 25/7 enjoys interacting with other people (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

When unbalanced, the number 25 takes on too many responsibilities and duties and feels that life is hard. Because of all the responsibilities, it never allows itself to rest and sets aside its passions and spirituality. In time life becomes gray and dull because of life's daily chores. 25/7 can be full of inner turmoil that it doesn't share with others.

In balance, the number stands for playfulness, adventure, and spirituality found through working in groups.

The Numerology of 34: Its Meaning and Significance

A central theme in the number 34 is connecting the spiritual with the material. You can create step-by-step systems to help yourself and others gain more profound wisdom and insight and can be a professional leader in a field, community, or business with a higher purpose.

34/7 has the life task to build a bridge between the metaphysical and the more down-to-earth and practical aspects of life. The number 34/7 gives an "expert" vibe to study a particular profession in-depth and become very good at it. In imbalance, there is a tendency to be withdrawn, melancholic, drown in emotions, or have different addictions (such as alcohol, smoking, or sugar) rather than using one's spiritual power to rise above the lower desires. In balance, the number 34 gives an almost priest-like vibration that attracts others due to a high level of spiritual wisdom coupled with more earthbound practicality that allows people to see how spirituality can be applied in daily life.

The Numerology of 52: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 52 is philosophical, popular, and curious about experiences and other people. When balanced, this vibration adds a great spirit of adventure, a sense of humor, warmth, and a feeling of universality.

52/7 loves to travel and visit new places - and its often unusual places. 52/7 could, for example, decide to travel to Gambia or Kazakhstan simply to see how people live and work in these exotic parts of the world.

52/7 energy can be inquisitive. When you sit opposite a 52/7 person, they might be looking at you and trying to gauge how you are feeling. The person may be trying to pry things out of you in all sorts of ways, and the more you resist, the more they try to get inside.

Where 25/7 is a fairly soft vibration on the outside, 52/7 is often a little more aggressive since it has the number 12/3 in its outer diamond used in the external world (the diamond chart for 52/7). It is a more "pompous" and competitive energy that wants to be on top, win arguments and appear strong and intelligent.

In imbalance, life can be full of problems that one thinks about in infinity. 52/7 is also known to be the most verbally aggressive of all the numbers, where it will continuously be testing you to find your weak points. Former President Donald Trump has a lot of 52/7 in his numerology chart and is known for his verbal aggression, bullying, and name-calling that he uses to tire and degrade his opponents.

## Numerology 61 Meaning
Planet neptune associated with the numerology of 61

The Gandhi Number

The digits in the number 61 have the digit sum 7 which resonates with the planet Neptune. 61 is often written as 61/7 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 61 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 7 here.

The Numerology of 61: Its Meaning and Significance

The 61/7 vibration is about consciously letting go of the material world to move into a spiritual path where one is filled with the joy of pure being.

Where 16/7 is an energy that often stands for suffering and loss - then 61/7 is a very conscious energy where you voluntarily can give up things or people that ultimately do not serve you any longer or who no longer contribute to one's further development.

61/7 is very focused on the principles for correct and spiritual living. It loves spiritual laws, spiritual systems, virtues, simplicity, minimalism, philanthropy, etc. For example, the great yogi and liberator of India, Mahatma Gandhi, resonates a lot with the numbers 16/7 and 61/7 in their high balanced forms.

In imbalance, there are too many principles pushed onto other people against their desire and a lack of joy in life. 61/7 can be afraid of losing loved ones and filled with anxiety and grief (like sister vibration 16/7). It can be overly dry, moral, and principled. It can be immersed in problems with the family and want to escape from the world which is perceived as a hostile place.

In balance, one understands the most profound spiritual laws and is supported in living in a free and simple way. The vibration is not typically used in the name chart as it can give a slightly "dry" vibe and is challenging to master.

Planet neptune associated with the numerology of 70

The Numerology of 70: Its Meaning and Significance

70/7 is a spiritual, cosmic, intuitive, and introspective vibration. It stands for the journey into the soul and reveals the hidden mysteries of existence. It analyzes problems on all levels of life and finds the most intuitive and profound solutions. It is a literary and scientific vibration that accumulates knowledge, which in time becomes wisdom.

The number 7 in 70/7 means that it is naturally interested in questions of faith and religion and has a natural interest in spirituality and universal laws. The 0 symbolizes both the cosmos, the divine, and eternity, and together with the number 7, it gives a great desire to research spiritual things.

Belief in the divine helps 70/7 to navigate through the sea of life. 70/7 gives a high intelligence where one accepts God's omnipotence and omnipresence and helps live a simple life that often follows a spiritual or religious precept.

In imbalance 70 can be surrounded by chaos, is super critical, and has little desire to be present in the world, which is perceived as a problematic place full of obstacles and low vibrations. It can be quarrelsome and wants those around it to take care of the practical matters as the world is seen as a burden with never-ending practicalities. It can have many problems in the psyche, especially in the first half of life. It can be overly serious, moral, and principled and judge other people. It can also be manic and ungrounded because imbalanced 7 and 0 can create a lot of mental energy and chaos.

In balance, 70 is good at letting go and lets universal intelligence carry out the work.

## Numerology 79 Meaning
Planet neptune associated with the numerology of 79

The Scientist

The digits in the number 79 add up to 16 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 7 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the planet Neptune. 79 is often written as 79/7 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 79 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 16 here and the primary digit sum 7 here.

The Numerology of 79: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 79 is a vibration that is often either super-spiritual or super scientific/atheistic. But common to these two seeming opposites is the ability to analyze and understand the underlying truth. For the atheist version of 79/7, there is almost a "religiosity" to its atheism where it has spent hours attempting to prove God's non-existence. And for the spiritual version of 79/7, it approaches religion almost scientifically, dissecting every paragraph to search for the deeper meaning.

Common for the religious zealot and the atheist scientist is a deep desire to understand how reality works (either physical or spiritual reality). So it categorizes and analyzes everything and is highly detail-oriented and thorough.

79/7 can be an extremely hard working vibration. It is willing to set family and the physical body's needs aside to reach lofty goals. Often family and bodily needs are deeply irritating facets of life, and the 79/7 will do whatever it can to outsource the practicalities of life to other people to devote itself 100% to its spiritual or scientific purpose. Few archetypes can work harder than the 79/7, who is known to sometimes have a 16-hour workday.

Billionaire Elon Musk has a chart full of 79/7 and is also known to be one of the hardest working people on Earth. He pursues his business and engineering with a relentless focus and doesn't stop until he achieves what he wants.

In imbalance, 79/7 can have a very harsh attitude where it judges itself and others. It hates other people's dogmas but is blind to its own. It thinks that its own ideas and attitudes are the only ones. It can convey its knowledge aggressively because it has an inner "right and wrong" syndrome. There can also be a flagellant attitude in the 79/7. Flagellants are practitioners of a form of self-punishment by whipping their skin with different instruments of penance. In its most imbalanced form, we have the energy of the Spanish inquisition, where "non-believers" were rounded up and severely punished.

When balanced, the number gives an awakened analytical mind that observes phenomena and extracts the essential essence and knowledge.

## Numerology 88 Meaning
Planet neptune associated with the numerology of 88

The Karma Burner

The digits in the number 88 add up to 16 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 7 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the planet Neptune. 88 is often written as 88/7 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 88 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 16 here and the primary digit sum 7 here.

The Numerology of 88: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 88 vibration gives great beauty, charisma, and the power of fascination. But it also gives a fearful nature where one is afraid of the consequences of one's actions. In the number lies a powerful lesson about learning from one's mistakes, from this or past lives. It is a number that is very difficult to master and can give many tests in life.

The number 88 is a number of karma and fate – both because it is a master number (that brings extra karma with it) and because we here have a "double-up" of the 8 energy, which symbolizes karma. And not only that, the digit sum of 88 is the number 16/7, which is also called a karma number.

88/7 can be found in the charts of people who have gone through immense suffering and mental anxiety but who often have transcended the pain to find new spiritual paths forward. Our research found that the leading combination of day of birth and lifepath among priests, bishops, and clergy, in general, is an 8 day (the 8th, 17th, or 26th) and an 8 lifepath. This shows that people of the church understand deep in their being the meaning of karma and atonement, lessons that come with the number 88/7. We also found that the leading day of birth and lifepath combination among founders was 88/7. A founder is someone who founds a school, or academy, or university – some sort of institution. This is because the number 8 resonates with eternity, and founders make something that still exists to this day, which makes founders resonate with the 8 energy (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

In imbalance, one may feel paralyzed and unable to take action due to internal conflict and doubt. There is often a feeling of walking a very thin line because veering off course might have unforeseeable consequences. The 88/7 can constantly vacillate between different choices and can never decide out of fear of the consequences. There is a feeling of "damned if I do, damned if I don't." The number 8 and digit sum 16/7 together can also create an extremely miserly energy where one holds on to one's money and resources due to fear. The 88/7 is burning the karma of many lives, giving a lot of fear, anxiety, confusion, and longing.

In balance, you become a master of analysis, able to see both sides of an issue and find your way through a jungle of opposites. There is beautiful and hypnotic energy in the aura of the 88/7 that fascinates others and can drop people dead in their tracks. It is a power of fascination that stops the mind and makes people completely freeze in wonder. In its highest hypostasis, the number 88/7 resonates with the universal laws and the Akashic records.

The number 88 is a master number

The number 88 is a master number meaning that the first digit is the same as the last digit. This means that the "inner lessons" (dictated by the inner digit) are the same as the "outer lessons" (dictated by the outer digit). So how we manage our inner life is reflected in the outer life.

One of the first lessons, when you have a master number in your charts (for example, a birthday number or essence number), is that you have to start mastering your thoughts and what vibrational frequencies you send into the world. What you think and focus upon should revolve around the balanced aspects of the inner digit (in this case, the number 8) and should be expressed out in the world through the digit sum of the number - in this case, 16.

The lessons of 88

Since this is the number 88 it means that your inner karma that you come with (8) aligns with your outer karma (8) - meaning you will burn a lot of karma in this life. For this reason life can feel very difficult (the imbalanced aspect of the 88 digit sum 16/7). The key is to start looking deeper - to develop a gaze that transcends the physical world and to look deeply into the world of cause and effect (8). Karma can be burned unconsciously - meaning all sorts of unpleasant things happen to you in order to teach you the lessons that you stubbornly refuse to grasp - or it can be burned consciously - meaning that you for example meditate deeply on everything that happens in your life in order to find the kernal/seed of resonance in your mind/subconscious which is causing those things to happen. When you start working in this way you can start penetrating your mind/body system in a very profound way. For this reason we see the number 88 in a lot of people who work very intensely with spirituality, for example through akashic record healing, past life healing or soul journeys.

## Numerology 97 Meaning
Planet neptune associated with the numerology of 97

The Spiritual Warrior

The digits in the number 97 add up to 16 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 7 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the planet Neptune. 97 is often written as 97/7 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 97 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 16 here and the primary digit sum 7 here.

The Numerology of 97: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 97/7 represents miracles, the transcendence of reality, and getting to the essence of things. 97/7 provides an understanding of the most existential aspects of existence. It is not afraid to go into all the recesses of the conscious and subconscious minds to explore the borders between life and death, light and darkness, emptiness, eternity, and truth.

Before the energy of 97 reaches its apex, it might have to go through a valley of deep existential anxiety and fear. But once that fear is embraced, understood, and surpassed, the number 97 becomes an unstoppable force. In this way, the number 97 can seem like a terrifying opponent as it has transcended its fear of death.

When 97/7 overcomes fear, it can easily live in "fringe fields" of life where others would feel unsafe. The ability to address fear allows the 97/7 to explore the depths of life and the deepest recesses of the subconscious mind, where it can find answers to some of the most profound questions of life: "Is there life after death?", "who am I?" "what is my true purpose in this incarnation?" etc.

In imbalance, there is the danger of using one's universal knowledge selfishly, as we see in the case of the "unscrupulous guru" who uses his followers to raise his status. It can go astray with a rampant ego. Its unpredictability creates a considerable fear in others. It can be cold and closed, has lost empathy with people, and can be violent and dangerous. 97/7 can be counterphobic to the extreme, meaning that it indulges in reckless behavior to go up against its fears. In certain stages of its development, it can be immersed in one life crisis after the other, where there is always grief, suffering, and a feeling of loss.

In balance, 97 is an incredibly deep and intuitive vibration and becomes a "spiritual warrior" who wins over its inner demons and becomes a beacon of light for others to follow.

Resonating with Vedanta, Buddhism, and successful spiritual practice

Understanding death at a deep level and can guide others in matters of life and death

Has overcome existential fear through profound awareness

A spiritual master who empowers others to find their essence

The 97 unbalanced is

Immersed in one life crisis after the other. There is always grief, suffering, and a feeling of loss

## Numerology 17 Meaning
The Star

The digits in the number 17 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 17 is often written as 17/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 17 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 17: Its Meaning and Significance

17 gives you an energy of "celebrity" and "stardom." It gives the charisma to be on a stage, shine like a star, tap into the deep archetypical currents of art and beauty, and communicate these aspects to humanity.

The 17/8 variant of the 8 is the most beautiful and spiritually minded of the 8's. It gives an "energetic" beauty that makes other people turn their heads and notice you.

In the symbolism of the number 17/8, the chakras are in perfect balance. This affects the body positively so that you have a good metabolism and a healthy hormonal balance. Many find that weight problems are resolved and find their ideal weight when they get the 17/8 energy into their name chart.

With the number 17/8, you can express common human or archetypal emotions through art or communication. Our statistics on over 100.000 celebrities reveal that the most common date of birth among actors is the 17th of the month. In general, 17 is the most common day of birth for entertainers. Many businessmen are also born on the 17th as the digit sum 8 is good for finances and money. We also found that next after the 25th, the 17th was the leading day vibration for writers.

In imbalance, it is a number of "negative spotlight" – meaning that people notice you when you don't want to be seen and that unpleasant aspects of your private life are revealed.

In balance, the 17/8 gives the energy of spotlight and beauty that makes you get noticed and shine on stage.

## Numerology 26 Meaning
The Protector

The digits in the number 26 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 26 is often written as 26/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 26 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 26: Its Meaning and Significance

26 gives strength, discernment, and the ability to balance energies, harmonize the social setting, and wisely judge any situation. 26/8 has a unique talent for administration that it takes on itself from a sense of responsibility. It may have exceptional abilities within accounting and bookkeeping or other types of administration where people are involved. There is also an innate talent for organizing significant events – for example, weddings, fairs, school get-togethers, and other social events.

Basically, 26/8 wants harmony and to be loved and appreciated by family, colleagues, and surroundings. But the external strategy to achieve this harmony, love, and appreciation is often to exude a subtle control over your nearest ones, often done by sprinkling them with love, compliments, or gifts.

Our statistical research has revealed the following interesting results regarding the number 26. Out of 646 founders (meaning people who establish an institution, school, or academy), the most common day of birth was the 26th. 26 is essentially a number 8, and the 8 resonates with eternity. Founders create something that stands the test of time. The 26th was also the most common day of birth among orchestra conductors, showing the 26s ability to administer many energies simultaneously. It is also the leading day of birth among people within intelligence and surveillance. Many CIA agents and spies have the number 26/8 in their charts (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

In imbalance, 26/8 becomes a number of control, which is presented as "nice" and "necessary" – but underneath this, we find a deeper motivation anchored in control and power. According to Chaldean numerology, the number 26/8 also resonates with both the giving and receiving of ill advice.

In balance, 26/8 is a number that gives an excellent talent for administration, observation, and tracking.

## Numerology 35 Meaning
The Chieftain

The digits in the number 35 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 35 is often written as 35/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 35 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 35: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 35 gives lessons of strength, influence, and personal power. When balanced, you are a "good" chieftain who protects and stands up for those around you or specific causes to protect vulnerable groups of people or animals.

Money, prosperity, power, and influence fascinate 35/8 because their life lesson is about acknowledging these things (if you have 35/8, for example, as your higher life path number). But it's a bit like a child might be fascinated by a weapon, that it avoids it out of fear. So even though there may be a soft surface or personality, there is an interior deeply preoccupied with success and power. This desire is sometimes suppressed, so the person with 35/8 can be surprised and afraid of this internal drive when it sees it.

Like sister vibration 53/8, the 35/8 can be very interested in social work that balances power in society. 35/8 can locate vulnerable groups and make a stand for these groups. We find the 35 combination in the charts of social activists, feminists, anti-apartheid activists, animal welfare activists, gay rights activists, revolutionaries, etc. You can find an extensive list of activists with a 35 or 53 combination in their charts below.

The series of numbers 3,5, and 8 are called "The money triad" because each number is good at creating wealth. The 3 creates wealth through strategy and expansion. The 5 creates wealth through communication and marketing. The 8 creates wealth because it understands how the economy is built up and how to grow money. Together these numbers are a powerhouse of wealth creation. For example, we can mention Jeff Bezos, who has 35/8 and 53/8 in crucial positions in his name chart.

In imbalance, 35/8 tends to be "tribal," warlike, power-hungry, and vengeful. You judge people harshly, and as a result, some become afraid of you. Your word becomes the law. Jeff Bezos also displays some of these qualities in his "empire." It is well known, for example, that people working in Amazon warehouses have deplorable work conditions. You do as you are told, or you are "out." In strong imbalance, we find 35/8 in very tribal-minded people who live on the fringes of society, such as mafia bosses, gang members, or Hell's angels.

In balance, you become a pillar of righteousness and strength who protects the weak and upholds justice.

## Numerology 44 Meaning
Master Healer

The digits in the number 44 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 44 is often written as 44/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 44 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 44: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 44 is a master number (master numbers have the same digits) that requires a lot of work to master the vibration to its fullest potential. 44 is about changing, healing, regenerating, and building.

It stands for hospital care, healing institutions, medical arts, and relief work.

There is a great deal of disagreement between Chaldean and Pythagorean numerologists about whether 44/8 is a good number. The Chaldeans and the Indian numerologists tend to avoid 44/8 like the plague. It is a number that foretells misfortune and loss of all kinds - wealth, reason, love, etc.

On the other hand, the Pythagoreans see it as an absolute "master number." They see it as an extremely rare treasure of a number that surpasses even 33/6 because it is not seen very often - there are not many dates that can be added together, giving a number as high as 44. The idea is that a master number contains the previous master numbers.

Both the Chaldean and the Pythagorean numerologists are probably right. At first glance, it is an anxiety-inducing number that involves a lot of hardness with the built-in 4-8 tension both internally and externally. But if you have the courage and patience to take maybe 10-20 years to attain the full benefit of the number, it is worth the wait. If you have the inner strength, you can get something ingenious out of the 4-8 combinations. But it is a long and hard road, and therefore 44/8 cannot be recommended as a name number in the name chart.

But as a life path number, 44/8, like all other numbers, presents a great potential.

When imbalanced, one can be over-focused on the goal and face tremendous resistance in life. 44/8 gives studiousness and a tendency to overwhelm yourself with work and study. You have to go through a long learning process to get the most out of the number. There can be a lot of anxiety over losing control with an overly complicated life, where one keeps repeating the same mistakes repeatedly. There can be physical and mental tensions that devolve into health issues. There can also be a tendency to constantly get distracted by outer things forgetting the essential focus.

When balanced, it gives an enormous amount of skill, intelligence, and ability to set oneself and one's lower desires aside to help and heal others.

Explore more about the numerology of 44 and its powerful implications in our online program "Chaldean Numerology for Beginners." Uncover every aspect of this archetype through the NP NUMBERS 1-99 ARCHETYPING SYSTEM™, detailed statistics, Tarot, Tree of life, and other numerological interpretations. Click here to learn more about Chaldean Numerology for Beginners and how you can start your journey towards deeper knowledge and understanding.

The 44 in balance is:

A master number that requires a lot of work to get the most out of

A healer number good with medicine, hospitals, and healing

Learning great karmic lessons that take it to the next level of wisdom

Solving the material problems of the world

Taking big complex goals breaking them down into smaller steps

The 44 unbalanced is

Overfocused on goals, studies too much and neglects the self

Anxious of losing control with an overly complicated life

Repeating the same mistakes over and over again

Having physical and mental tensions that devolve into health issues

Always distracted by outer things forgetting the essential focus

The number 44 is a master number

The number 44 is a master number meaning that the first digit is the same as the last digit. This means that the "inner lessons" (dictated by the inner digit) are the same as the "outer lessons" (dictated by the outer digit). So how we manage our inner life is reflected in the outer life.

One of the first lessons, when you have a master number in your charts (for example, a birthday number or essence number), is that you have to start mastering your thoughts and what vibrational frequencies you send into the world. What you think and focus upon should revolve around the balanced aspects of the inner digit (in this case, the number 4) and should be expressed out in the world through the digit sum of the number - in this case, 8.

The lessons of 44

Since this is the number 44, it means that your inner desire for transformation and healing (the number 4) is best channeled by building institutions and systems that will stand the test of time (the number 8).

In imbalance, there is a tendency in this number to feel like the black sheep, to long for things to be different, and to feel like a victim of circumstance (number 4 imbalanced).

The number 44 can be a complex number to master since the numbers 4 and 8 tend to repel each other. The key is to structure and channel the rebellious and contrarian nature of the number 4 into a system of thought which can "handle" that there are many deviations, innovations, and explorations on the way.

If the outer structure is solid (8 balanced), it can handle all the fluctuations that the number 4 brings. This dynamic between rebellion and innovation (4) on the one hand, and structure, focus, and diligence (8) on the other hand, if understood and handled in the right way can lead to geniality.

## Numerology 53 Meaning
The Civil Rights Activist

The digits in the number 53 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 53 is often written as 53/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 53 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 53: Its Meaning and Significance

The 53/8 vibration is more balanced than its sister vibration, 35/8. 53/8 is about taking your power and strength back and becoming full of confidence and joy. You fight for the things you have dear and stand up for your cause.

53/8 has a considerable drive and talent to go in and add or change aspects of the social structure so that everything gets better and a little more functional in the long run for everyone. This may be concerning their own social circles or at the societal level. The number 8 in 53/8 can immediately detect an imbalance in a social structure or a power relationship.

Please take a look down below at the section called "Civil rights activists with 35/8 and 53/8 in their charts". We find all kinds of social activists with 35/8 or 53/8 located in different pivotal positions in the chart, ranging from civil rights activists, human rights activists, freedom fighters, revolutionaries, animal rights activists, anti-apartheid activists, women's rights, etc.

The numbers 3,5 and 8 are called "the money triad," giving the 53/8 good money-making skills. Money translates to value and, thereby, self-confidence on an emotional level. 53/8 in balance has a high self-confidence and a great authority in its demeanor and appearance.

In imbalance, the 53/8 is territorial in a very narrow sense and becomes tribal. It protects a small band or gang and has intimidating energy towards anyone, not part of its clique, tribe, or gang. It can be very greedy and use its power for its own selfish gain. 53/8 can be obsessed with themes of power and can have sadomasochistic tendencies. It has not yet understood the lessons given by 3,5, and 8, and either uses money and power selfishly or is afraid of money and power.

In balance, it protects human rights and gives a voice and power to those who don't have one.

## Numerology 62 Meaning
The Family Leader

The digits in the number 62 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 62 is often written as 62/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 62 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 62: Its Meaning and Significance

The 62/8 vibration is about family, rules, and emotions. There is a tendency to organize the family and take care of them. Occasionally maybe a little more than they want. 62/8 loves order and tradition and can feel very insecure when these things are missing. Organization is used to manage the environment and others and organize own emotions. 62/8 will do anything to defend loved ones.

62/8 also has a very strong patriotic streak. It values the motherland, old traditions, and the community.

In imbalance, 62/8 tends to be controlling, and like its sister vibration 26/8, there is a tendency to give and receive ill advice. Like 26/8, the number 62/8 resonates with surveillance and keeping an eye out that people behave themselves. It can have too many principles in life and love, which can annoy others. There is also a tendency in 26/8 to accumulate destructive emotions and hang on to grudges and resentments; it is not so good at forgiving others for perceived transgressions.

To better understand 62/8, we recommend reading about the number 26/8.

## Numerology 71 Meaning
The Magician

The digits in the number 71 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 71 is often written as 71/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 71 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 71: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 71/8 gives a sense of magic and an enchanting charisma where others notice you. With deep finesse and fine-tuning in preparation, you create magnetic products or creations. The 71 vibration is a reversal of the number 17/8, the Star, but in 71/8, there is more focus on having total control over the magical, charismatic aspects. Where 17/8 is a channel for the divine energy, then 71/8 is the one who creates the magic. With deep finesse and fine-tuning in its preparation, it makes fascinating products or creations. In its deepest essence, 71/8 is about ILLUSION. What is illusion, and what is reality. This question occupies 71/8 on a deep level.

For example, the illusionist David Copperfield is born on a 7 day and has life path number 1, giving a subtle 71 diamond between the day and life path. David Copperfield is known for building grand illusions that stun the audience.

Another example is renowned escape artist Erik Weisz (aka Harry Houdini), who has a 71/8 "brand diamond" in his name chart (specific numbers that dictate how other people see you). Houdini would meticulously prepare the stage and the illusion so that he was sure to escape the seemingly impossible situations that he was trapped in.

In imbalance, 71/8 can suffer from spotlight allergy like its sister vibration 17/8 (we invite you to read the article about 17/8). It can be selfish, individualistic, lonely, and create a lot of emotional drama around it. There is also the danger of falling for one's own illusions so that reality and fantasy are mixed up in an unhealthy way. 71/8 can potentially be narcissistic and psychopathic when extremely unbalanced.

When balanced, the number 71 gives acting talents and the magician's magical touch, controlling the act of magic and keeping the audience's attention.

## Numerology 80 Meaning
Divine Power

The digits in the number 80 have the digit sum 8 which resonates with the planet Saturn. 80 is often written as 80/8 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 80 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 80: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 80/8 contains the number 0, which adds a cosmic element to the energy of the number 8, which elevates the 8 into its spiritual aspect. The 0 helps to marry the material aspects of the 8 with its spiritual, uplifting, and refined elements. This union results in magical and occult powers that can be used in anything from art to business or influence society.

80/8 is a number of enormous divine strength. When unpacking the number in the diamond chart, it reveals other numbers of immense power. 80/8 can fascinate and hypnotize and can stop all evil in its tracks. It has a special relationship with time and can also help the bearer slow down or literally "stop" time.

80/8 loves the merging of the spiritual and the material – which is why the number 80/8 loves the following:

Long-lasting projects on the physical plane that embody a spiritual element – for example, the building of a church

Beautiful objects that have a story behind them

Resonance with things that last "forever" – for example, stories with archetypes and how the archetypes intermingle and interact

Occult traditions and systems

Unique places that are infused with an energy of spirituality and mysticism

80/8 is a stalwart; it can work for many hours non-stop.

In imbalance, the 80/8 is intimidating, unpopular, and creates fear in others. It can be evil and occult in a negative way. It is the tax collector archetype that over-administrates others and overworks itself to the point of death.

The number 80 does not appear in the charts as the relationship between two positions because no position has the number 0. It can occur as a big personality number, but this is rare since the big personality number is made from the consonants in the name, and few names are long enough to add up to 80/8.

A cosmic power that can stop all evil

Powerfully hypnotic and mesmerizing and can stop the mind

Building long-lasting structures that stand the test of time

Is having deep and personal transpersonal spiritual insights

The 80 unbalanced is

Celebrities with 80 in their numerology chart

Big Personality number 80

There are a few interesting people who have an 80/8 big personality number, and one of these is Joseph Stalin, one of the greatest dictators of all time. Stalin embodied many of the qualities of the 80/8 for good and for bad. He stabilized the USSR with politics and grand-scale projects. He hypnotized and terrified people precisely like the cosmic power 80/8 would.

The biographer Montefiore writes, "it is clear from hostile and friendly witnesses alike that Stalin was always exceptional, even from childhood." Stalin was a complex personality with profound self-control and memory (qualities of the 80/8). He could soak up enormous amounts of information. He studied every aspect of Soviet life - film manuscripts, architecture, military hardware, etc. This is another aspect of the 80/8 energy; it can remember and see everything in its mind's eye. In some form or the other, Stalin held a near-perfect mirror image of the Soviet Union in his mind. His private life and working life were merged entirely, and he never took any days off from work. The ability to thoroughly combine personal with work life is another 80/8 quality. At the same time, Stalin was a complete tyrant and terrifying force who readily sent thousands of people into work camps and exile if he suspected the slightest bit of dissent.

## Numerology 89 Meaning
The Archetype Artist

The digits in the number 89 add up to 17 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 8 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the planet Saturn. 89 is often written as 89/8 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 89 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 17 here and the primary digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 89: Its Meaning and Significance

89/8 is about artistic genius, refined emotions, and aesthetic sense. It gives the ability to master something to the fullest and perfect it. 89/8 has occult capabilities, esoteric knowledge, and a deep understanding of different kinds of archetypes and their personality structures. 89/8 pulls spiritual truths and ideas down to earth and displays this to other people.

89/8 can shine light into other people and sense energies. It can read and understand symbols and intuitively understand the different symbols used in art, culture, and media.

It also has many of the same qualities as the number 78/6, "The occultist," but the karma and the responsibility weigh more. 89/8 gives a beautiful and fascinating charisma that hypnotizes people and is often found in the charts of actors, models, and artists.

Due to digit sum 17/8, the 89/8 can suffer from spotlight allergy, a huge fear of being on stage and in the limelight. This spotlight allergy can evolve into a fear of being seen, of being stalked, and that the public can follow along in your most private matters (see 17/8 explanation).

In balance, the 89/8 energy gives an intense spotlight energy that puts you in the limelight in a harmonious way and where you have the inner strength to deal with all the energy that being in the spotlight attracts.

## Numerology 98 Meaning
The Michelangelo Philanthropist

The digits in the number 98 add up to 17 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 8 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the planet Saturn. 98 is often written as 98/8 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 98 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 17 here and the primary digit sum 8 here.

The Numerology of 98: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 98 is the lesson of eternity and building things/projects that "last forever." Everything you do is measured up against a larger purpose. You should effectively be doing what it takes to get to the next level — great art or big projects that raise awareness.

98/8 is one of the most charismatic and spellbinding numbers. Outwardly it makes beautiful and timeless products. Inwardly it goes into the depths of its being to find the right inspiration and answers. It is a number that often experiences "divine interventions" – in the form of inspiration it suddenly downloads from the Cosmos – or through things that happen in the outer world that confirm an idea it has been turning in its head.

The 98/8 is in contact with an ancient splendor, traditions, and magnificence that it wishes to channel through any medium, usually art and music. The themes and motives that the 98/8 displays in its work have a sense of something timeless and something that will last forever.

We find the number 98/8 in the charts of great philanthropists and artists. For example, the artist Michelangelo has 98/8 in his numerological psychic self chart (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

In imbalance, one can feel immersed in problems and attract negative attention (imbalanced digit sum 17/8). There is also a tendency to take on too much on one's shoulders (inner 8 and digit sum 17/8) because there is a strong inner vision of how things should be. There can also be a fear of choices which leads to not making decisions. The 98/8 becomes paralyzed with anxiety can not move forward in fear of the consequences. 98/8 can remember so much and hold so much information from its "eternal memory" (8 and 17/8) that it can be perceived as wise but challenging to understand.

In balance, 98/8 is one of the most beautiful and high vibrating numbers that resonates with sublime art and eternal archetypes.

## Numerology 18 Meaning
Planet mars associated with the numerology of 18

The Warrior

The digits in the number 18 have the digit sum 9 which resonates with the planet Mars. 18 is often written as 18/9 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 18 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 9 here.

The Numerology of 18: Its Meaning and Significance

18 gives a considerable drive and power to accomplish all the actions that it starts. The number 18 is the most militant and straightforward of all the Mars numbers (numbers with digit sum 9 – i.e., 18,27,36,45,54,63,72,81, etc.).

The number 18/9 indicates a determined and hardworking personality with great vision and a strong masculine awareness and drive. You can accomplish great things and achieve incredible wealth through mental focus and enterprise. You are individualistic and your own boss.

18 gives good leadership skills and an invincible drive. It is the day vibration of many political leaders. Our statistics reveal that the 18th is the leading day of birth among prime ministers. The job of prime minister is a very "hands-on" and action-oriented leadership job, as opposed to, for example, "president," which in many countries is more ceremonial in nature. We also find in our statistics that the 18th is the most common day of birth among academics. The discipline and mental focus of 18/9 is apparently good for plowing through many books and knowledge.

The 18/9 is never used in new name charts. The number is too hard, fanatic, and choleric and tends to shut down the softer emotions such as love, empathy, and sensuality.

In imbalance, the number 18 is conflicted, angry, choleric, and fanatic. Many with 18/9 energy seem happy, light-hearted, and open on the surface, but there is nothing light about the inner life of 18/9. There is a lot of guilt, shame, fear, and insecurity. For example, there can be a lot of internal conflict with a shifting attraction and repulsion to money and power.

In balance, the number 18 has energy, focus, willpower, and drive.

## Numerology 27 Meaning
Planet mars associated with the numerology of 27

The Scepter

The digits in the number 27 have the digit sum 9 which resonates with the planet Mars. 27 is often written as 27/9 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 27 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 9 here.

The Numerology of 27: Its Meaning and Significance

27 is a number of leadership that gives you strength, overview, and an ability to lead teams. You are good at seeing other people's qualities and how they can contribute. This ability allows you can put together the best combinations of people for specific tasks, a skill that can lead you far as a leader.

Like all numbers with digit sum 9, the 27/9 is an aspect of masculine consciousness. The number 9 gives willpower, focus, direction, and discipline. The 2 and 7 are both quite soft and feminine numbers, so the number 27/9 leads in a gentle and people-oriented way full of compassion and the ability to listen and take in feedback.

Our statistics show us that the number 27/9 indeed is a number of leadership. It is a powerful business number as well, and we see that many of the top CEOs and owners of companies are born on the 27th of the month. It is also a powerful number for politicians that need to show leadership skills. In our research on 6594 politicians, we discovered that the second most common day of birth was the 27th of the month, the top place being taken by the number 20 (Numerologist PRO Statistics).

In imbalance, the 27/9 tends to mentalize everything (the number 7 is very mental). To access the truth of the heart, 27/9 must let go of what others think of them (imbalanced 2 and 11/2) and learn to trust their own emotions. A tremendous potential ability and a great life lesson for 27/9 is awakening the heart and learning to listen to what it says—the "whisper of the heart," which you can only hear by silencing the mind.

The number 27/9 is called "The scepter," which stands for a leadership ordained and protected by the divine powers.

In the symbolism of this number, you create peace between people and nations. It might live in a mansion, but it will feel terrible if the neighbors have bigger mansions.

When balanced, it brings personal power and strength, intelligence, leadership, and the ability to create something significant.

The Numerology of 72: Its Meaning and Significance

The 72/9 vibration is about wisdom, professional skills, and thoroughness. We have here a very humanistic number that desires the well-being of mankind and which has a great spiritual access to life. 72/9 is focused on finding solutions. It has great compassion and a sense of community.

72/9 is a number where the outside world is carefully scrutinized to discover what is right and what is an illusion. This leads to many inner conclusions that ultimately lead to knowledge and wisdom (the number 9). Therefore, this number is also called "the professional leader." One becomes a master of "life and death" through intense observation. You learn about the processes of life, both through what you see and through the knowledge you acquire through books. Due to the inner number 2, this knowledge is translated into a kind of knowledge that can be used for the good of the community.

In imbalance, there can be fanatical tendencies, and one can feel distanced from others. There is also a tendency to escape into one's own inner world of studies and research and forget to give back to the community. 72/9 tends to hold on to old dogmas out of internal inertia and laziness, and it can have extreme and outdated views that it imposes upon others. It can also judge others out of fear of losing status or control. 72/9 can also have dry interests that it chooses over the community that it lives in.

In balance, the number 72/9 is one of the most humanitarian numbers and resonates with the "freedom, equality, and brotherhood" idealism that came forth with the French revolution.

## Numerology 90 Meaning
Planet mars associated with the numerology of 90

Divine Wisdom

The digits in the number 90 have the digit sum 9 which resonates with the planet Mars. 90 is often written as 90/9 where the digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 90 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the digit sum 9 here.

The Numerology of 90: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 90/9 is about going to the essence of things. It is the number of the cosmic warrior who is always in a state of full action and activity - but at the same time, a "channel" for a universal cause.

The number 90/9 represents the cosmic dance between man and the universe - a dance that contains both action and non-action, all at the same time. It resonates with "Nataraja," which depicts the Hindu God Shiva as the divine cosmic dancer or "lord of the dance." He dances within a circular arch of flames, symbolically representing the celestial fire that in Hindu cosmology creates everything and consumes everything in the cycles of life. His face is completely tranquil even though he is in the midst of the continuous chain of creation and destruction.

In imbalance, this number tends to be uncompromising and to say things directly without considering the feelings of others. One may seem fanatical, but this is often because a person with an "ordinary" level of consciousness cannot see the divine source behind the actions inspired by the number 90/9.

In balance, the number 90/9 resonates with "Wuwei," meaning action without intent (from Taoist philosophy), and also the state of "karma yoga" (from Hindu philosophy), where you can work without expecting the fruit of your actions, which in this way are given to God. Through karma yoga, you become a channel of the divine will.

The 90 in balance is:

A cosmic warrior who acts out the will of God

Downloading heavenly wisdom into its heart and mind

A master of "Wuwei" – acting without will or intent

A master of karma-yoga – allowing the Divine to work through it

A number of "divine wrath," where anger is used in a divine way to rectify the wrong

The 90 unbalanced is

Forgetting about its divine integration, and becomes manic

Taking the fruits of the action and growing the ego

Hurting others with its militant actions that it doesn't explain

Causing a lot of chaos and havoc because it doesn't inform others about its actions

Needlessly destructive

## Numerology 99 Meaning
Planet mars associated with the numerology of 99

The Finalizer

The digits in the number 99 add up to 18 called the secondary digit sum, which again adds up to 9 called the primary digit sum which resonates with the planet Mars. 99 is often written as 99/9 where the primary digit sum is placed after the slash. To understand 99 more in depth we recommend you also read more about the secondary digit sum 18 here and the primary digit sum 9 here.

The Numerology of 99: Its Meaning and Significance

The number 99/9 is about completing one's life lessons, goals, dreams, and actions, whatever the price. Sometimes completion becomes the goal in itself. 99 is the end of a cycle and represents the completion of the ultimate goal. 99/9 resonates with "doomsday" – the end of the world. Called "Ragnarok" in the nordic traditions, or "Maha Pralaya" in the Hindu tradition.

It is self-discipline in its utmost consequence. It represents ultimate strength and courage from the triple 9 energy (9 + 9 = 18, an aspect of 9).

We find the double 9 combinations in the charts of people who have an extreme self-discipline in any field, whether it, for example, is the famous saxophonist John Coltrane who practiced for 12 hours per day (born in a 9 month in a 9 year), or the cyclist Lance Armstrong who won Tour de France 7 times (born on a 9 day, in a 9 month in a 9 year) or a person with an intense daily spiritual practice – for example, Hildegaard of Bingen, (born in a 9 month in a 9 year). We also have Colonel Sanders, the founder of Kentucky Fried Chicken (born on a 9 day, in a 9 month, in a 9 year), of who it is said that he was turned down 1009 times before he heard his first yes. 99/9 stands for finalizing, and in this sense, it is also interesting that the last apartheid president of South Africa, F.W. de Klerk, who "finalized" apartheid, was born on a 9 day and has a 9 life path number.

In imbalance, 99/9 can be misunderstood by other numbers who cannot keep up with one's actions, which can seem principled, lofty, and maybe even "tough." There can also be a tendency to shut out emotion or discount it with the number 99. Emotion is sacrificed on the pyre of principle and reaching the end goal.

99/9 can be a number of great karmic and spiritual tests, because it signifies that we are on the very last part of the journey, the part with the most obstacles and trials, and 99/9 has to be strong to push through the very last leg.

Because of the perceived or actual difficulties, 99/9 can be tempted to resort to unethical methods to reach the final destination (for example, the aforementioned Lance Armstrong, who resorted to drugs to win Tour de France). The tired 99/9 so much wants to reach the end goal that the goal justifies the means. 99/9 can also resort to making everything in its life automated. Rules and schedules take care of everything, and everything becomes soul-less. It can feel so tired deep in its bones that it is waiting for its own death because the goal, though right around the corner, feels so far away that there is nothing left to live for. And that's the irony with 99/9. Sometimes it walks 99 miles of the 100 miles but gives up at the 99-mile point, not knowing that it was that close. And here we have a valuable lesson with the number 99/9 - sometimes in life, we feel that there is no hope left and that we are at the end of our tether, when in fact, the light is just around the corner. So next time you feel that you can not go on a single step, perhaps you should give it one last shot.

In balance, the number gives power, strength, drive, and the ability to complete any task.

The 99 in balance is:

Finalizing all actions and bringing them to their divine closure

The highest potential of humanity

A very old soul that knows everything there is to know

The 99 unbalanced is

A number of great karma and spiritual tests

The goal justifies the means. Resorts to unethical methods to reach the final destination

Everything is automated. Rules and schedules take care of everything, and there is no soul

Completely closed down and cannot feel its emotions

Waiting for its own death because it feels there is nothing left to live for

The number 99 is a master number

The number 99 is a master number meaning that the first digit is the same as the last digit. This means that the "inner lessons" (dictated by the inner digit) are the same as the "outer lessons" (dictated by the outer digit). So how we manage our inner life is reflected in the outer life.

One of the first lessons, when you have a master number in your charts (for example, a birthday number or essence number), is that you have to start mastering your thoughts and what vibrational frequencies you send into the world. What you think and focus upon should revolve around the balanced aspects of the inner digit (in this case, the number 9) and should be expressed out in the world through the digit sum of the number - in this case, 18.

The lessons of 99

Since this is the number 99 it means that your inner desire to reach the pinnacle of the mountain will be met in your outer life by huge obstacles which symbolize the final things you have to achieve in order to reach transcendence. The digit sum of 99 is 18 which is a number of battle, war, fanaticism and action. This is not an easy number to master due to the many obstacles which have to be passed. The only way through the obstacles is through "karma yoga" - meaning you give the fruits of all your actions to God. Only in this way can you attain the necessary inner "lightness" to perform all action EFFORTLESSLY. When your remove the burden of expectation from your mind you allow divinity to work through you and a whole new level of energy and power enters your being.
